Jen looked alarmed “Who’s that?” she asked Neil.
He said “That means I’ve got to go, I can’t stay”
“Who is it?” Jen asked again
The knock came again, but was more insistent this time.
“I can’t tell you, not right now at least” Neil answered getting up to leave.
Jen grabbed at Neil “When will we see you again?” she pleaded.
“I don’t know, it’s not easy at all, but I’ll make sure I do, just be ready” he said
Jen nodded in acceptance and called over to Clara “Clara, come here and say good bye to Neil”
Clara came over and said “Goodbye” and held out her hand for Neil to shake.
Jen and Neil smiled together at Clara’s attempt to be an adult, Neil took her hand and said “Nice to see you again Clara, I hope we can meet up again sometime soon”
The door opened and the nurse barked at Neil “Time’s up, get out”
Neil gave Jen one final hug, waved at Clara who was back hiding being Jen’s legs and walked out of the room. The nurse closed the door behind him and said “I’d run if I was you”
Neil bolted out of the office to the lift, which was open and waiting for him, he punched the carpark button and slumped against the mirrored wall of the lift as it dropped down the floors. In no time the doors opened to the carpark and Neil rushed out to where Vex had parked. 
She was gone. Did he stay longer than he was given, he wasn’t sure time had stopped in that Doctor’s office. 
A car horn honked behind him making him jump, he turned and it was Vex. She was waiting with the door open just around the corner from the lifts, Neil jumped in and they sped out of the carpark.
#
Vex didn’t say anything once Neil jumper into the car, she concentrated on moving through the traffic and getting out of the area as quickly and smoothly as possible. Neil was thankful for the silence, he needed time to work through his feelings after his short reunion with Jen and Clara. 
He shaking a little as came down off the heights of elation seeing them after so long, the relief in learning Jen had not abandoned him was washing through him..
Yet at the same time the anger that he’d tried to suppress for years was bubbling to the surface with a ferocity that he didn’t feel like controlling. He knew his life was ruined, but to hear that Jen and Clara had been punished for something that had nothing to do with them set him off. Casting them out of the home he and Jen had built together and being sent down as Level 5 restricted citizens meant they’d had lost everything. What made it worse is that someone had lied to them both, the questions of who was behind it and for what purpose was burning in Neil’s thoughts.
Vex still wasn’t talking and Neil wanted some answers, but he was mindful of what she, and the Greys, were able to achieve on such short notice, time and again. He simply said “Thanks”
Vex smiled and said “All part of the deal Neil, you delivered for us, so we delivered for you. I hope it went well?”
Neil answered “You told me you couldn’t find any evidence they tried to contact me, yet my wife told me she tried for years, including after I was released” He let his statement sit there an accusation that needed an answer.
If Vex noticed the accusation, she wasn’t taking the bait “Neil, we couldn’t find any official records of your wife, Jen is it? making contact. I doesn’t mean she didn’t try somehow, but nothing official was lodged” Vex said whilst keeping her eyes on the road.
“You made me thing she really didn’t want to see me, why the fuck would you do that?” Neil asked.
“Neil, we just didn’t know one way or the other, better to prepare you for the worst.” Vex said.
“Fuck you” Neil said flatly.
Vex chuckled “ Neil, that’s entirely the wrong attitude to be taking, especially when I’m the one who can help you find out who did this to you. Don’t you want to know and perhaps even exact a little bit of payback?”
Neil didn’t hesitate “Just tell me who they are and I’ll rip them to shreds” his anger was now full formed and looking for a target.
Vex smiled sweetly, she knew she’d reeled Neil in “Hold on to that, we’ve got some work to do before we start ripping heads off and you’re a big part of it. Sounds like you’re all in?”
“I am” said Neil.











CHAPTER 13

FUGITIVE (JIM 6)

Jim didn’t know where he was going, once he left his old neighbourhood he drove around the city for hours. He needed to work out what to do, he was now a wanted fugitive being chased by the Police and the media. Every time he tried to grab control of his life back he’d been blindsided by something else, one escalation after another. His last 24 hours had been a blur of what felt like never ending escapes and Jim had no idea what to do next.
At the very least he was still free, which gave him options, not that he could see any real options in front of him right now. The Grant’s car was anonymous and unremarkable, just another mid-range EV effortless gliding through traffic with the windows tinted to thier maximum shielding Jim from the outside world. He felt safe for now, eventually the Grant’s would see thier car was no longer in the garage and report it stolen, Jim could only hope they were enjoying thier holiday too much to even bother looking, after all cars simply weren’t stolen in thier neighbourhood. The battery was full and it would last more than long enough, he just needed a place to go.
Jim had so many questions, the one person he knew who could help answer them was Shane. He had a place to go, Shane’s apartment.
Jim didn’t understand how or why Shane’s phone number was disconnected, but thinking about it today Jim decided he would probably do the same. It was just too risky for a Level 9 to be caught associating with a Disruptive Person who was now a Level 5 Restricted Citizen. Shane was his friend and had connections that could put at least some of this right, he admitted to himself that lessening the damage was about all he could hope for. If he could just see Shane face to face Jim was sure he’d help.
Jim set the route to Shane’s building, it wasn’t far as he’d been skirting around the edge of the central city district for a while now blending into the traffic on the inner ring roads. He’d been sitting in silence since he left the Grant’s garage, but now he had a destination, his thoughts turned to what was happening in outside the quiet solitude of the car. 
Out of habit he asked the car to turn on the news radio, but nothing happened. shaking his head at his own stupidity, this wasn’t his car, Jim activated the radio manually. 
It was the end of the financial report “…Pinnacle Analytics shares are up 7.5% on news of it acquisition of social media points monitoring start-up LevelUp. Pinnacle CEO Lucien De Montfort said of the acquisition that Pinnacle Analytics was always looking to bring innovative concepts into the family and LevelUp’s real time social media points tracking system would be game changer for citizens and would add to Pinnacles marketing leading platform. In other news Nova Corporation announced thier new data centre was now fully online providing it with computational capacity not seen since the before the Great reclamation… ”
Jim muted to volume, it was what he used to listen to most mornings, the mention of Nova Corp priced his ears though. They’d dropped him like he never existed, there was something nagging him about the whole Nova deal, he wished he’d never heard from them in the first place, none of this would have ever happened.
The car had arrived at Shane’s building, but unlike his last visit here there was to be no automatic entry into the carpark, Jim was going to have to leave the relative safety and anonymity of the car and go up to the front door in person. 
He parked about a block away down a side street and sat in the car, he told himself he was just waiting for the foot traffic to slow down a bit, but in reality he was trying to gather courage to leave the car. He looked around the car and saw a cap on the back seat, at least that would offer him a small amount of privacy, but Jim knew he could be spotted by any number of cameras so he’d have to walk very carefully.
He took a deep breath and got out. Jim pulled the cap down as low as he could to try and hide his face from the building cameras as skirted along the side of the building towards the entrance. He quickly stepped inside the public foyer of Shane’s apartment building and off the street where Jim felt exposed, anyone might recognise him at any time. 
The foyer was empty, no one was manning the small concierge desk, it was outside peak times, so Jim the space to himself. With his held held low Jim moved over to the intercom system and buzzed Shane’s penthouse on the 47th floor. It was a longshot, but he knew Shane didn’t start work some days until lunch time.
The buzzer was answered by a male voice he didn’t recognise “Hello”
“Hi, is Shane in?” asked Jim.
“Sorry, who’s this?”
“I’m an old friend of Shane’s, is he still in this morning?”
“No Shane here“ came the reply. Damn it, he must have left for work.
Jim tried a different approach “Look, I’m sorry to bother you, I was here for dinner a few nights ago and I left something being, Shanes said I could pop in and grab it, could you just buzz me up.” If he could get upstairs he could wait for Shane to come back.
“Sorry, there’s no one here, I can’t help you”
Jim pressed “Shane said it was ok, I’ll only pop in and grab my stuff, be only a few minutes”
“No, I don’t think you understand, this place is empty, no one lives here, I’m on the painting crew redoing the place for the new owners” said the man on the other end of the intercom.
“What do you mean there’s no one there? When did they leave?” asked Jim.
“Pal, I can’t help you, my crew has been here for a few weeks, I have no idea who was here before that, all I know is I’ve got to finish this job so I’ve got to go.” the intercom connection was ended.
Jim stood there silently looking at the dead intercom terminal, yet again he was caught off guard. A few weeks, the man had said, that means just after thier BBQ Shane had left. Had he even lived there in the first place? Jim had so many questions, but one thing there was no doubt of now, Shane was gone. 
Jim was jolted out of his thoughts as the the front doors slid open, the noise from the street outside bled into the near silent foyer as a couple sauntered in. Jim couldn’t stay there any longer and risk being recognised, he bolted out of the foyer before the doors slid shut again and kept going until he reached the safety of the Grant’s stolen car.
For the second time today Jim drove off without knowing where he was going, he just needed to get away and work out just what the hell was going on.
#
He didn’t know how he got there, he hadn’t even thought to make the trip, but Jim found himself parked a few doors down from Sarah’s parent house. He wasn’t sure what he would do if he saw Sarah, she’d made it clear she didn’t want to be associated with him as a Disruptive Person. 
Only a few days ago Jim had harboured some hope he’d be able to reconcile with Sarah after he found a way out of this mess. He’d since had resigned himself to the fact the his path forward was going to end up with him in Jail. 
Part of him wanted to see Sarah just one more time and remind her that thier bond as husband and wife was real and built over years or climbing the ladder as team. To convince her thier love wasn’t something to be thrown away and to beg her to stand with him. 
The more realistic part of Jim knew it was over, that part of his life was likely gone forever, he just wanted to see her one more time before he’d end up in jail.
Jim looked down at the console on the car, it was just after lunch now and Sarah wouldn’t likely come back from work for hours yet, was he really going to just sit here waiting Jim asked himself, probably, he thought.
There was a quiet knock on the passengers side window. Adrenaline rushed through Jim’s body, had he been found by the Police or the media. The car’s tinting was turned all the way up and it wasn’t registered to him, so that couldn’t be it. 
The knock came again more insistent this time. Jim cracked the window open without disengaging the tinting and asked “Can I help you?” trying hard to avoid being recognised by the man standing outside.
The man said calmly “No Jim, but I can help you.”
Hearing his name Jim went cold, it must be media, but how they’d tracked him down he didn’t know. Before Jim had the chance to respond the man spoke again “Open the door Jim, we need to talk and we’ll both get noticed if I stand out here any longer, oh and don’t even thing of driving off” the point was made as a gun barrel was poked through the opening in the window and pointed directly at Jim.
He had been thinking about just flooring the accelerator, but he’d have to take the car out of park mode and he didn’t think he was fast enough to beat a bullet, so he unlocked the door.
The man opened the door and sat in the car with a smooth fluidity that matched his tall wiry frame, Jim could tell he was someone not be messed with. He kept the pistol in his hand, but placed it in his lap not exactly point at Jim, it was close enough to make the point that Jim was not out of danger.
Jim asked “Who are you?”
The man answered “First things first Jim, lets go for a drive. I was waiting for you to come here and if I can work you were coming here, so can anyone else, so let’s go now” He waved forward with the pistol as if to emphasise the point.
Jim pulled out and drove past Sarah’s parent’s house without even looking at it, his mind was solely focussed on not getting shot.
Jim asked “Where are we going”
The man answered “Nowhere in particular, just get on the ring road and drive around the city for a bit, it will give us time to talk.”
Jim did as he was instructed to do, he started to selected a random destination on the other side of the city when the waved him off “No need for auto pilot today, just keep it in manual mode thanks”
Jim did as he was instructed and kept his hands on the steering wheel and drove the car, something he wasn’t really used to doing for more than a few minutes at a time, he felt awkward and more than a bit nervous making his was through traffic himself.
 The man spoke again “If you’re wondering who I am, you can call me Rook, I might be able to help you get out of the bind you’re in.”
Jim asked “Help me, so why are you pointing a gun at me?”
The man chuckled “Well Jim, you’ve had a rough couple of days and people in your situation can act irrationally, I’d prefer if you didn’t” He looked down at the gun “This keeps you nice and polite”
Jim had navigated onto the ring road now, he was warming up a bit to driving himself, his white knuckle grip on the steering wheel had relaxed a bit. Not too much though as Rook’s menacing gaze was surveying his every movement, the pistol held in his lap ever present, he was right, it kept Jim subdued quite well. He had to accept that he was trapped in the car with Rook for now, his plan was simple. Keep calm and when an opportunity to escape presented itself, take it.
After a few minutes of silence as Rook let Jim concentrate on driving, Rook spoke again “So Jim, quite an adventure you’re had the last few weeks, we took particular interest in you when you little outburst at Apex, quite the show.”
Jim was ashamed of that day, it was the day he’d ruined everything “I think everyone in the city has seen that” he said.
Rook agreed “Yes, I’ve seen it many times, of the interesting things about the video was these so called Grey’s who released it. Do you know anything about them Jim?”
Jim shook his head “I’ve never heard of them before, despite what the News says, who the are they?”
Rook answered “Apparently they’re a group of disaffected people, mostly level 4 and 5’s, who hate the system and want to break it or something like that. A sentiment that might resonate with you right now huh?”
“I don’t want to break anything, but I’d like to find those people why did they choose my life to ruin” spat Jim.
“I’m sure you would. You know one of other the interesting things about that video is there’s no mention of who you were at lunch with, don’t you think that’s a bit odd?” Rook asked 
Here we go thought Jim, the real reason why we’re here, just an unscrupulous reporter of some sort pushing the limits to get a scoop, this guy wasn’t getting anything from Jim.
“No, it doesn’t matter, it was all me anyway” Jim said.
Rook smirked “Not sure why you’re covering for them, they dropped you like a rock as soon as the video hit didn’t they. Don’t worry about giving me a name, we already know who was at lunch with you.”
Jim just shrugged “Ok, so you know names, why are you in this car then?”
Rook didn’t answer, instead he said “Take the next exit please.” 
Jim didn’t really have a choice Rook’s eyes never left Jim and the gun never moved away. He took the exit, he knew where they were going now, to the Outer.
#
The next ten minutes were mostly silent as Jim took directions from Rook. They drove out of the affluent suburban sprawl of the Green and into the increasingly drab grey cityscape of the Outer. Jim grew up out here, but it had been more than twenty years since he’d ventured this far out, he wasn’t entirely comfortable going back. He’d worked all of his life to earn enough points to leave the level 4 and 5 grifters and no-hopers of the Outer behind for good, it felt like failure to be back on these streets.
Rook told Jim to pull into a small multilevel carpark next to one of the Outer’s garishly decorated shopping malls. ”Here we are, drive down to the bottom level”
Jim drove down the ramp into the dimly lit basement level of the carpark.
“Over there by the black van” said Rook.
Jim brought the car into the parking spot and as soon as he’d stopped Rook opened the door and got out without saying a word.
The sliding door of the black van opened and Rook got in, almost immediately followed by a a dark haired woman stepping out of the van and quickly getting into the car.
She closed the door and turned to Jim and smiled a cat like smile, it wasn’t warm or comforting, Jim felt a lot like prey being sized up. At least she wasn’t holding a gun, but that didn’t make Jim feel any safer.
“Hello Jim, you’ve been a hard person to track down” She said.
Who was this new person sitting in his car uninvited Jim thought, he simply said “And you are?”
She raised her eyebrows “I’m Vex, nice to meet you Jim”
“Hello Vex, I’ve been forced to drive here staring down the barrel of a gun, maybe get to the point of this little trip” Jim was getting frustrated in being handled like he didn’t have any say on what was going on.
Vex smiled that cold smile again and said “Jim, I can understand that, and I know you’ve been through the wringer in the last few days, so I’ll get right to it” She looked around as if this someone might be watching them, satisfied they were alone down here in the basement carpark, she continued “Jim, I’m guessing right about now you got a lot of questions and want some answers?”
“Sure” said Jim, not committing to anything.
“Good, what if were to tell you that the video that put you here in this car today wasn’t released by the organisation called the Greys. Instead what if I were to tell you that it was released by Nova Corp themselves?” Vex Asked.
Jim shook his head, that just wasn’t possible, he responded “How could you possibly know that? There’s absolutely no reason Nova Corp would do that”
Vex looked directly into Jim’s eyes and held his gaze “I can know that because we are the Greys and we didn’t release the video, as for Nova Corp, we’ve got some theories”
Jim was shocked, this person sitting in the car was one of the so-called Greys, the group who Jim had vowed to find and exact his revenge on. She was calmly sitting in the seat next to him denying it all. Jim knew he was in danger, but his anger was rising.
Vex saw it, “Yes Jim, get angry, but at the right people. Lashing out at me will only get you shot and dumped in the Floodway. “ She pointed over her shoulder to the van, the door was still open and Rook was sitting watching them, his gun pointed directly at Jim.
Jim shifted in his seat, he was angry but had to hold it in, he had no doubt they’d follow through with thier threat. He needed more information and he asked “Ok, suppose that’s true, can you prove it?”
Vex pulled out a small tablet from inside her jacket and opened up the screen to show Jim a Video. 
Jim watched and immediately could see the video was a feed from the security system at the Apex restaurant, he could see the staff moving around setting up tables in the empty restaurant.
He asked Vex “What am I looking for here, so far all you’re showing me is a empty restaurant”
Vex answered simply “Keep watching, were getting to the good part”
The video continued and Jim could see the table he’d been sitting at when he had lunch there a few weeks ago now. Then he saw it, Rob Hughes, the Nova corp executive who’d invited him out that fateful day, came into view and stood at the very same booth they’d be in for thier lunch. Rob took something out of his pocket and spent a few seconds placing it in the plants that were tastefully placed on the dividers between the booths. Satisfied with whatever he’d placed, he sat with his back to it and beckoned one of the waitstaff over. It was the same waitress that had  red wine over Jim. 
Rob had a good conversation with her, he could see Rob pointing to the empty seat across from him, he picked up an empty glass and then acted out spiling it over the table. It dawned on Jim what was going on just as Vex fast forwarded the video, scrubbing through a good 10 minutes by look of the time-code.
Vex slowed the video down and Jim saw himself walk into view and saw Rob and himself shake hands enthusiastically before both taking a seat in the booth. The video stopped and froze on Jim and Rob sitting here at the start of what would be the beginning of the end of Jim’s life as he knew it.
Jim sat frozen in his seat in the car mirroring the video, he was trying to process what he saw and what it all meant.
Vex looked intently at Jim gauging his reaction, after moment she spoke “That proof enough?”
Jim was still unable to to put it all together in one coherent timeline in his mind, all of his assumptions of what had happened to him over the last few days had been ripped away and scattered to the winds, literally nothing he thought was fact was anymore.
Vex nodded as she could Jim processing what it all meant and put the tablet away.
“Jim, they set you up, this man that you know as Rob, planted the camera, set up the waitress, got you drunk and then led you down the path to your own fall.” Vex said matter of factly.
Jim shook his head to clear it, he felt groggy like he’d been punched. “I don’t understand, why would he do that? Why would Rob and Nova do this?”
“Well, we have our theories on that, but we do know they likely targeted you well before the lunch, perhaps even months before. You’re not the first person to be caught up with these people” Vex said.
Jim was shocked “What do you mean: months?”
Vex put up her hands in mock submission “Whoa Jim, I don’t have all the answers. What I really want to know is are you willing to help us get them and bring these guys down?”
Jim didn’t hesitate, he badly wanted to know who these people where and why they’d targeted him, and most of all he wanted to know if his disappearing friend Shane was involved. “Yes, fuck these people, fuck them completely”
Vex smiled, she got what she wanted.
“Good Jim, we’ve got some things to take care of before we start as you said: fucking them completely. Rook is going to take you to a safe place for now, sit tight there for a bit and we’ll speak again.”
Jim nodded, he was still reeling from all he’d learned. 
Vex got out of the car and jumped into the van to be replaced by Rook in the passenger seat, this time there was no gun in his hands. 
The dirty black van backed out of the car spot next to them, Jim watched in the car’s mirrors as it drove up the ramp and out of the basement, leaving Rook and Jim sitting alone in the car again. Both inside the car and the carpark outside were silent giving Jim a brief moment of solace.
Rook spoke up, his demeanour total changed from the trip there “Let’s get out of here Jim, we’ve got a bit of a drive ahead of us”
Jim pulled out of the car space and drove out of the basement as if auto pilot himself. His mind was still churning, but in the turmoil of his thoughts there was a growing resolve to fight back now against these people who had torn his life apart. Rook gave him directions, but he didn’t care where they were going now.







CHAPTER 14

THE SCAR (JIM 7)

Jim drove Rook through the city mostly in silence, only the occasional direction from Rook broke the quiet inside the car. The silences gave Jim the time to examine his situation. He wasn’t sure he was safe with these people, he’d need more proof of thier claim to be the so-called Greys and thier other revelations about his former life as a corporate executive. He resolved to be alert for an escape route in case he needed it.
Jim still couldn’t quite understand why Nova Corp would have set him up like this, he was just one of millions of trying to climb his way up the citizenship ladder and secure a better future. Sure, he cut some corners and bent the rules when he could, but nothing anyone else wouldn’t have done, so why did they choose him? He needed answers, but all he’d got in the last few days were more questions.
They wound thier way through the city for the next half hour or so skirting the blurred line between the Outer and the Green, never venturing too far out. They didn’t drive in a straight line, Rook had Jim circle back a few times just in case someone might have picked up thier trail, but no one had, the car wasn’t connected to Jim in any way.
Eventually Rook told Jim to pull over into an empty side street. “This will do, we’ll leave this car here” he said.
Jim pulled over, Rook immediately pulled out a what looked like a battery unit, it was about the size of an old brick but wrapped in black tape, there was a long red cable coiled around it. Jim didn’t see him bring that into the car, he must have got it in the van when they stopped to speak to Vex. Without asking Jim to move, Rook leaned over the centre console and started fumbling around with the plug end of of the cable attached to the battery. Jim moved his legs out of the way and said “Whoa, what are you doing, what is that?”
Rook was looking for something under the console “There’s a diagnostics port somewhere under the steering wheel, I need to plug this into… dont just sit there it’s easier for you to do it”
“What are you doing?” Jim asked again.
“We’re leaving the car here, but we need to clean this system first, this will wipe the car’s memory unit” said Rook.
Jim understood, he felt around under the steering wheel and almost immediately found it “Here it is” he said to Rook, who passed him the plug.
Once it was plugged in securely, Rook pressed the only switch on the box, the centre console and the dash immediately stuttered and went blank, a faint smell of burning plastic and electronics started to seep into the cabin.
“Should it do that?”Jim asked
“Nothing wipes a system like a massive burst of electricity, this little booster will fry just about any circuit” said rook as reached under the steering wheel and unplugged the cable.
The smell of burning was growing stronger by the second, Jim was looked across at Rook, who raised his eyebrows at the tendrils of smoke now starting to seep out from under the dashboard. “Of course too much juice and you can set off a chain reaction, we might want to get out. Now”
Jim didn’t need to be asked twice as both of them jumped out of the car that was rapidly filling with smoke. 
The black van that was parked in the basement garage pulled up next to Jim and Rook, the side door shooting open before it had stopped. Rook jumped in without hesitation, Jim paused which elicited a rebuke from Rook “Fucking get in, we have to go!”
Jim jumped in and before his feet even hit the floor of the van it was darting forward making him stumble backwards into Rook. There was no sign of Vex in the otherwise empty rear of the van. He straightened up in time to look back out through the rear window as the van sped off, he could see flames curling up the side of the Grant’s car from the battery pack underneath. 
Very soon it would be engulfed in flame, a fire that like all battery fires couldn’t be put out. He felt a bit guilty seeing the car burning like that in the disused side street. He knew the Grant’s well and he was genuinely sorry for firstly stealing the car and then getting it destroyed like that. 
Rook was watching it burn just Jim was, they turned around a corner and lost sight of it forever. He had a satisfied smile on his face, looking at him, Jim suddenly knew burning the car to the ground was the result Rook wanted, the thought didn’t fill Jim with much comfort, had he just made his situation worse?
#
The van weaved another circuitous route through the city, however this time with every turn and circle back Jim could see they were steadily moving further away from the glittering city centre. They had long since left the Green and were making thier way through the Outer, from what Jim could see they would end up hitting the city’s edge soon.
He asked Rook “Where are we going?”
Rook, who was leaning back against the van’s side sitting, like Jim, on one of the two uncomfortable fold down benches running down the sides of the van, didn’t bothering opening his eyes “We’re going to a safe house, somewhere you can stay whilst we work out your next move”
“We work out my next move? not sure I agreed to that” Jim said.
“I doesn’t matter what you think, we saved your arse and we get a say in what you do next… or we can just let you out here and you’re on your own”.
Jim actually didn’t want that, so stayed silent, Rook kept his eyes closed and didn’t elaborate further.
Eventually the van pulled over, Jim and Rook got out onto a largely deserted street, there were no cars parked, no people walking, most of the shops were boarded up, those that weren’t boarded up certainly didn’t look open. A smell of chemicals and rot wafted into Jim’s nose “what is that awful smell?” he said aloud.
Rook scrunching up his face in disgust at the same smell said “Get used to it, we’re only a few blocks from the Scar, lets get inside, it’s better in there.” as he walked into one of the dilapidated townhouses that lined the streets.
Jim of course knew what the Scar was, it was a vast mile wide floodplain that separated the city from the factories of the Rust Belt. It was a reminder of the consequences of war.
The Scar was a toxic floodplain scouring a wide trench across the landscape from north to south for hundreds of miles. The result of the war of Reclamation, a global total war that was waged with both conventional and environmental weapons. The attack that created the Scar released billions of litres of water and toxic waste in one of the largest natural disasters ever seen, it changed the shape of the land and the course of civilization.
Citizens learnt about it in grade school, but the vast majority never ventured close enough to smell it. Jim was one of those, even though he grew up in the Outer, going out to the Scar was just not something people ever wanted to do, too many urban myths of deadly toxic fogs killing people living close to the scar.
He followed Rook inside and they walked straight through the townhouse into the small backyard, and then straight through a gate at the back of the yard into the next house townhouse behind it. They went up the narrow stairs to the first floor and into a small bedroom, it was sparsely finished and as dour as Jim might have expected it to be. 
Jim was about to ask if this was his room when Rook put his finger to his lips to be silent. He quietly ran his hands over the wall and with a small push and a click the wall opened up a completely hidden door to the townhouse next door. Rook and Jim stepped through, this room was the opposite of the sparsely furnished room they’d just left.
It was much larger as the internal walls had been removed creating an open plan room. At one end the windows to the street had been bricked over, to Jim’s professional eye he estimated it had been done at least 50 years ago, judging by the old bricks used. The room itself was furnished with bunk beds, a couple of tables, two old couches and a kitchenette along one wall. Jim thought it could house at least a dozen people, but today it was empty.
Rook waved expansively at the room and said “Welcome to you new home, at least for a little while” He could see Jim was unconvinced.
“If you’re worried about being close to the Scar, don’t be, the deadly fog stories are old wives tales, at worst it smells for a day or two, but being in here keeps most of the smell out.”
Jim wasn’t entirely convinced, the truth seemed to be a variable concept to Rook.
Rook continued “I’m not staying, I’ve got places to be, someone will be along soon enough, there’s supplies in the kitchen, the bathroom is downstairs and whatever you do, do not leave this building.” 
“How long until someone else gets here?” asked Jim
“I don’t know, at some point I guess. Anyway, I’m off, remember don’t leave, a safe house is only safe if you stay in it” with that, Rook disappeared down the stairs leaving Jim alone. 
Jim sat on one of the couches and contemplated his situation, he was alone in a safe house as far out of the city as he’d ever been, beholden to the Greys for his safety, a wanted fugitive on the run. Somehow despite all this he felt like he had taken a little bit of control back and bought himself some time to develop options. He sank back into the couch, just then he realised higher bone-tired he was. Within minutes he was fast asleep.
#
Jim woke cold and stiff on the couch to a quiet and darkened room, he didn’t know how long he’d been asleep, but judging by his his stiff neck it had to have been a few hours at least. He sat forward groggily and looked around the room he’d been left alone in, his blood ran cold as he saw someone sitting at one of the tables quietly starring it him. That woke him up quickly.
The figure was obscured by the darkness of the room, it didn’t move, was Jim dreaming it?
A voice broke the trance “So, you’re our new quest I take it?” It was a female voice, but not Vex. She got up and walked over to the light switches and flipped them on.
“I thought I’d let you nap for a bit, seems like you needed it” she said as the lights illuminated a tall and muscly woman.
Jim had questions “Who are you, how long have you been sitting there?”
She didn’t answer, instead went over to the kitchenette and stirred a big pot that was on the stove. It was at that point Jim registered the smell of the cooking, he was hungry, the aroma of the cooking got his stomach rumbling. It was also at that point he realised none of that was there when he came in, this person must have come in and prepared the stew whilst Jim was asleep. He shook his head and admonished himself, that could have been a very costly mistake.
“Hungry” She asked as she ladled what looked like stew into two mismatched bowls and walked them over to one of the tables
Jim was very hungry and without a word joined her at the table.
“Sorry it’s not much, but out here the best we can get is the Government minimum protein, so tonight and every night in fact, we’ve got VatSoy, supposedly chicken flavour. Don’t worry I’ve put as many spices in there as I can to mask the taste” She said and started to tuck into the stew. 
Jim sniffed at the plate, it seemed ok, he’d never eaten VatSoy a few times in his life when he was young and ran out or credits for the month. He tentatively took a spoonful of the stew, it had a spicey yet vaguely chemical flavour, he didn’t detect any chicken there, but his stomach overruled his head and he ate it.
“Seems like it does the job” she said as she watching Jim wolf down a few spoonfuls.
For Jim it wasn’t so much the taste, it was the warmth and the sensation of it filling his stomach, VatSoy was designed to feel nice on the palate and fill you up cheaply. Jim asked “So, who are you? Rook didn’t tell me who was going to meet me here.”
“Rook’s not a big one for words is he, you can call me Corra, I’m what you might call the Manager here, everything that goes on in here, who comes and goes, who gets to stay.” she said pointedly looking at Jim.
“So I get to thank you letting me stay here?” Jim asked.
“Temporarily, until we work out what to do with you” Corra said.
Again with the royal “we” deciding what to do with me thought Jim, it didn’t sound any better from Corra as it did from Vex or Rook.
“Do you have any ideas what that might be?” Jim said probing for more information.
“I don’t know, I’d say you’ll have to lay low for a few weeks, if not months, before the heat comes off your story. The reality is that the rest of your life will be off the grid now, we’ll find something for you to do, but that’s just me guessing, it’s up to the boss really” said Corra.
Hiding for months was not what something Jim was interested in doing, what he really wanted was to clear his name and get answers about Nova Corp and he couldn’t do that rotting away in this Safehouse.
“You mean I’ll have to stay here for months, when do I get payback on those pricks at Nova? Isn’t that what you people do?” Jim was getting agitated at the thought of staying here indefinitely.
Corra not noticing or caring Jim was raising his voice said calmly “No, you’re not staying here for more than a few days, a week tops. You’ll have to keep moving around to be sure no one is tracking you.”
“And what about Nova Corp?” asked Jim.
“Easy there, we’re not looking to get into a fight with a player as big as Nova just now, we need to build up to that kind of action.” Corra answered as she finished off the stew in her bowl and got up to take her plate back to the kitchen.
This was not the path Jim was expecting to take after his conversation with Vex, he thought the Greys took action, but it seemed they just sneaked around in the shadows. 
Jim changed tack “Rook said I shouldn’t leave, is the place being watched or something?”
“We can’t rule that out, but probably not, no one wants to come out here. This is technically not even in the Metro Police’s jurisdiction, there are so few people out here.” Corra said over her shoulder as she washed the bowl.
“So I can leave if I want to” it was more of a statement than a question.
Corra stopped and turned around to look at Jim directly and said “Jim, like it or not you’re ours now, we’ve taken you in and it was not without some serious risk to our organisation. If it was me I’d be very grateful and wait for them to come up with a plan for you, Vex is not a person to cross. Let’s just say the Scar holds more than a few skeletons.”
Jim was less and less happy being the Grey’s new plaything, he wasn’t going to wait around in the safe house for them to tell what he could and couldn’t do.
“Fair point, I’m going to hit the bed for real this time, and one I should use?” he asked.
Corra waved at the bunk beds at the other end of the room “Any of them, you’re our only guest tonight.”
Jim grabbed a bunk furthest away from Corra in the kitchen and got in the bed, it was old but at least it was clean. Jim didn’t go to sleep immediately, he lay there thinking though his options, soon enough he’d made up his mind and drifted into a deep sleep, satisfied he knew what he needed to do when he woke up.
#
He’d been awake for at least an hour now, there were no windows in the room so he couldn’t tell what time is actually was. He felt rested, or at least as much as he could expected to be since he fled his house. He was also apprehensive, he was going to leave the safe house today one way or the other. 
He didn’t want to be anyone’s plaything and he wasn’t prepared to wait around for these people to decide his fate, he was going to take his future into his own hands. Slowly and quietly he looked around the room, he looked past the bunk beds where he was into the open area where the tables and lounges were, it was dark and empty.
He looked to the kitchenette all the way at the other end of the room, there was nothing cooking there was no sign of Corra. Jim got up, being careful to avoid making any noise he walked to the top of the stairs that went down to the ground floor. He was causally looking around the room as if he was trying to find his way in an unfamiliar house, but in reality he was just looking for cameras. He couldn see any obvious ones though. He stopped and listened intently at the top of the stairs, he didn’t hear any sounds coming up from downstairs and as stealthily as he could he descended.
Downstairs all the walls seemed to be intact, the bottom of the staircase led into a small hallway dimly lit by sunlight bleeding in around the edges of the window on what Jim assumed was the back door. The windows downstairs hadn’t been bricked up, merely painted over. Jim snuck over to the door and tried the handle, it was locked, it couldn’t be that easy could it thought Jim.
He saw the bathroom door was open just a few steps away and he went inside to avoid being seen trying to open the door.
When Jim left the bathroom a few minutes later Corra was waiting in the corridor for him with a smile, it wasn’t exactly friendly, she said “Morning Jim, no need to sneak around on my behalf, I was already awake. Now let’s grab some breakfast upstairs there’s some people coming in soon to work on your next move” she motioned for Jim to go back up the stairway.
This was the moment thought Jim, more people were coming and he might not get the chance again. He stepped aside as if going around Corra to go up the stairs, crucially it put her in between him and the open door to the bathroom. Jim stepped next to Corra and shoved her, as hard as he could into the bathroom. She was caught totally unprepared for the shove and barrelled into the bathroom without any control smashing into the wall at the back of the room and falling heavily on the tile floor.
Jim slammed the door shut behind her, he deliberately stood back and kicked the door handle, his foot bounced off. He lined up another kick and the handle snapped off the door with a satisfying crack. He grabbed the spindle that was exposed and pulled it out so Corra couldn’t open the door.
Corra was banging on the door now yelling at Jim “Let me out, Jim don’t do this!”
Jim turned his attention to the door with his adrenalin pumping he launched a two footed kick to the door to get it open. It was stronger than he’d anticipated and he just bounced off. Jumping straight up he kicked at the door, once, twice and a third time, still the door didn’t budge.
Corra was doing the same, the banging on the bathroom door was now a rhythmic beat as she tired to kick the door open from the inside. Jim could she she’d beat him to the punch, he needed another option. He looked around and saw the broken door handle on the floor, quickly he picked it up and threw it as hard as he could into the window into the door. The glass shattered, the gaping hole let the early morning sunlight stream in.
The smashing of the glass stopped Corra’s kicking, she yelled from the other side of the door to Jim “Jim, think about what you’re doing, if you leave you’ll be on your own, we won;t take you back, Vex will hunt you down, Jim, are you there?”
Jim didn’t answer, Corra resumed kicking the door, the frame cracked as Corra, it would only be a few more kicks and she’s be out. Jim had to move now.
He pushed the biggest of the broke shards of glass out of the frame and climbed through to the small backyard. He clambered over the side fence as he heard the door inside burst open.
Jim didn’t know where he was going, but he hurled him self over three more side fences until he came landed in a laneway. He sprinted into the street and turned away from the townhouse not looking back to see if he was being followed, just wanting to put distance between him and the safe house.
He saw another lane way and ducked into it, Jim was panting hard now as the adrenalin emptied out of his system. He needed a place to recover, the laneway went all the way across to another street, he sprinted for it and quickly turned the corner. This street was a bit different, the town houses it empty shops. He couldn’t run anymore, his legs were like lead and he was seeing little stars in the corner of his eyes. He saw the boards on one of the shops was loose so he squeezed his was in behind it inside, pulling it close after him. 
The inside of the shop was just an empty space, the only furnishing was the dust on the floor. His legs totally spent, Jim slid down against the wall in a heap, laying on the dusty floor listening for any sing of pursuit. 
After a moment, he shifted slightly a sharp pain stabbed in his leg and he looked down to see his pants red with blood. Gingerly he examined himself, there was a slash in pants was oozing out blood. He must have cut himself climbing through the window. Jim ripped the cut in his pants open to see just how badly he was cut, it wasn’t too deep and had started to close up already, but lying on the ground as he was it certainly wasn’t clean. 
He slowly got up to explore the shop and see if he could get some water to clean the cut up a bit, the sound of a car stopped him dead. This area was abandoned so the only car the could be out there was the Grey’s looking for him. It drove past without stopping and Jim took in a deep breath.
He’d have to patient and methodical to get out the area, but he was now even more determined to get back to territory he was familiar with, that would give him at least an even chance of survival. That meant getting back into the heart of the Outer where he grew up and disappearing into the crowd.
#
Jim stayed in the old shop for a few hours, what looked like the same car drove up and down the street periodically, at least as far as Jim could tell from peeking at it through a crack in the boards covering the shop windows. However, it hadn’t been up or down the street for a good while now. He’d managed to find an old sink in the back of the shop with some running water and cleaned out the cut in his thigh as best he could. It should probably have some stitches, but being clean was all he could do for now. He’d have to get some antiseptics on it soon though.
Jim knew he couldn’t stay in the shop forever, he really needed to put some distance between him and the Greys before any more came in to help search for him. Jim just wasn’t in the mood to be cautious and wait a few more hours for night to fall before getting out of there, the smell coming in off the Scar was getting worse and he wasn’t sure it was all harmless.
When he scouted the empty shop he saw the back door opened on to a small car park out the back, it was as good a place as any to start. Jim paused at the door mustering up the courage to make the move and potential risk being seen in the daytime. 
He crept out of the shop into the little carpark, he could see the fog was deepening, along with the stench of chemical rot. Testing his leg he jogged down the small back street to the main road, it seemed ok for now.
The street was empty, Jim turned against the direction of the incoming foul smelling fog and jogged out onto the street.
The next hour was a tense stop and start jog, he periodically stopped in side alleys and deep door alcoves to check if he had been seen. A couple of times he heard cars but none closer than a few blocks away, the fog made them sound like the were right next to him though.
The fog cleared the further he went disappearing altogether along with the dilapidation of the streetscape. Eventually he saw some signs of life as shops were no longer abandoned and houses were no longer boarded up. Old cars and truck now frequented the streets as humanity pushed up against the abandonment and unlivable spaces of the Scar’s fog zone.
Jim was dirty and sweaty and his pants were ripped and bloody, out here on the fringes of the Outer he didn’t look too far out of place, just another Level 3 down on his luck. His hands went to his belt buckle where he’d taped the two untraceable credit sticks with tape he stole from the Grant’s basement, they were still there thankfully.
He ducked into the first convenience store he came across, it was more of a general store and had a bit of everything, but no clothes. The garish shopping centres where Level 4 and 5 citizens could spend thier government mandated credits were still some distance down the road in the Outer proper.
Jim grabbed a new cap to replace the one he’d left behind in the safe house and went to the front counter, the clerk didn’t react to his state of dirtiness.
“Hey man, you don’t have any pants here do you ”Jim asked, trying to slip into an Outer accent.
The clerk looked at for a second before answering, the accent clearly not helping Jim blend in. “No, sorry, no pants here, just the cap today?”
Jim pressed “Nothing out the back? I fell over and ripped them”
“Sorry, we don’t sell pants, just the cap?” he asked again.
Jim had no choice but to play his hand here, he pulled out one of the credit sticks and slapped it onto the counter “I’m not asking if the shop stocks them, I’m asking if you have any you want to sell to me.”
The clerk looked at the stick, he knew what it was and Jim could see the greed spark in his eyes. 
 “If that’s what I think it is I might have something for you, wait here” He left the counter and went out the back, Jim waited for either the clerk to come back of sirens from the metro police turning up to arrest him.
It was the clerk who came back, hold a pair of black pants, they were clearly store uniform. “I’ve got these, they should fit you, going to cost you 500 credits.”
500 credits thought Jim, they should only cost 50 credits or so. “500? Come on man, that’s steep.”
“Yeah well if that credit stick is what I think it is, 50 for the pants and 450 not to call the police. Deal or not?” he snapped back.
Jim didn’t really have a choice. “Throw the cap in and we’re good.”
“Sure, whatever.” the clerk said as he pulled out an old credit transfer terminal from the under the bench. Whilst technically not illegal, you wouldn’t want to be caught with one. That would be a signal to the Metro police that you might be laundering credits, and they did not like the idea of not knowing were every credit was being used. It was clear that the shop’s register was not going to be used for this transaction
The clerk keyed in 500 credits and Jim tapped his stick onto it, a quiet beep and a green light showing the credits were now sitting on the terminal. The clerk then tapped a similar stick taking the credits, there would no trace of them coming from Jim. 
“Want a balance?” He asked.
No fucking way thought Jim, you’re not finding out how much I’ve got on there, he simply said. “All good man, It’s probably tapped out after that. Got a place to change”
The clerk pointed over to the office door without saying a word. Jim went in and changed as quickly as he could, he had the feeling the clerk wanted more of his credits, perhaps the whole stick in fact. He donned the new cap and left the store walking away briskly so the Clerk would get any ideas.
Jim walked the streets deeper into the heart of the Outer and felt safer, he was less conspicuous and in familiar territory, it hadn’t really change in the past twenty or so years since he left. He knew the credit stick worked and he knew he could find a place to stay that didn’t ask questions. For the first time since Nova Corp had released the footage from the Apex restaurant, Jim was in control.










CHAPTER 15

NOVA CORP (SHANE 1)

Shane sat in his office reviewing papers on his ornate dark wooden desk. The office was not only huge, as big as ten or so normal offices would be, it was also beautifully appointed in the way that real old world money always did it. Dark wood lined walls, tasteful plush carpet, antique pieces of furniture, a vase with freshly cut flowers on a copper pot stand. Nothing was extravagant or garish, all the pieces of furniture and artwork were exactly where they needed to be, not too much or too little. That each piece was worth more than most people would ever earn in a lifetime was just a minor detail.
The office was silent, outside the large picture window, a blue sky could be glimpsed through the canopy of the old trees, casting dappled shadows over the immaculately maintained gardens and pathways beneath. 
Inside only the low murmur of his terminal playing a news feed was making a sound, the anchor on screen introduced a new story “…now for an update on the case of missing former Level 8 executive, Jim Dalton…”
Shane stopped reading and looked up at the mention of Jim’s name, he keyed the volume on the terminal so he could hear it better.
“…for an update we’ll cross over to Kira Maddox who’s been following the story for us since the video of the incident at the exclusive Level 8 only Apex restaurant was released by the so-called Grey movement, Kira.”
Kira was in the studio standing in front of a huge digital display wall silently playing highlights of Jim’s story. “Thanks Lina, right now Jim Dalton is still unaccounted for, after managing to evade arrest by a joint Metro Police and CEB operation at his house, there have been no signs of Mr Dalton for going on four days now.” Video of the Police breaking down the front door of Jim’s house was showing on the digital wall behind her.
“We now have some facts that paint a very concerning picture of a potentially very dangerous individual. For example, we now know that at the same time the Police were entering his house, Mr Dalton stole a car from his neighbours which was subsequently found completely burnt out some twenty-four hours later across the other side of the city. Forensics analysts from the Metro Police tell me it was a deliberate battery cell overload and question how a business executive would know how to do that without some sort of help.” Video of the Grant’s burnt-out car was now showing on the screen.
She continued “In breaking new just released today, the Metro Police tell me they discovered a very high tech chip modification on Mr Dalton‘s TV in his house, one that allowed access to citizenship restricted material.”
Shane involuntarily uttered the word “Shit” under his breath.
The News story went on, Lina the anchor now asking questions “That’s a very interesting development indeed Kira, what do you think that means?”
“Lina, when you add up how Mr Dalton was able to escape arrest by highly trained officers from both the Metro Police and the CEB, the fact he was able to set off the battery overload and now the revelation that he had highly sophisticated contraband chips in his TV at home, it paints a worrying picture of a dangerous and well connected person.”
“It certainly does, are the Police any closer to finding Mr Dalton?”
“No Lina, they are telling me privately that Mr Dalton has completely disappeared, however they are getting leads from citizens all over the city claiming to have seen him, some even from as far out as the Scar’s fog zone if you can believe that. They tell me there are methodically checking each lead and are confident they will track Mr Dalton down quickly”
“We all hope so, I know I’ll feel better once he’s safe and in custody” said Lina.
Shane tapped the keyboard to mute the sound, he sat silently contemplating the the update about Jim.
There was a knock on the office door, Jim looked up to see Rob Hughes standing in the doorway. “Hi sir, I’m, here for our 10am project briefing.”
“Yes, come in, good timing Rob. First lets grab some coffee” Shane said as he gestured to the antique sideboard loaded with fresh fruit, pastries and a beautifully ornate copper coffee machine.
#
Rob and Shane each sat on one of the two beautifully upholstered lounges in the centre of the room, the remains of their morning pastries and half full porcelain coffee cups on the low table between them. 
Shane was reclining on his couch savouring the last bite of his pastry, Rob wasn’t feeling so relaxed, he’d savoured the exquisite coffee and pastry, but he knew some hard questions were coming.
Shane sat forward and spoke “It’s always nice to stop and enjoy the little things in life Rob, there’s just such a primal pleasure to a simple moment of satisfaction, a hot coffee and fresh pastry is as good as any drug, don’t you think?”
Rob answered “Yes sir, I’m not going to disagree with that”
“No, I’m sure you’re not. Now what have you got for me?”
Rob picked up the paper files he’d brought in with him and shuffled through the files looking for one in particular.
“I thought in light of the news reports this morning we’d start with that project?”
Shane waved Rob off “No, we’ll keep that one for last this morning”
Rob put the file down on the coffee table and opened another file “Let just go in order then” Shane nodded in agreement.
Rob continued “Project 26, we’re in the third phase of that now, but I don’t think it has legs. The subject is displaying a lack of motivation to move forward.” 
Shane held out his hand and Rob passed the file over to him. He quietly flipped through the pages for a few moments, then said “Fifty-fifty on this one, apparently the marital bond is not as strong as we though it was here. Give it one more push, if that doesn’t work, just cut  the subject loose.”
Rob nodded “Will do, next is project 27, unfortunately it’s failed, we got outplayed by a rival company in the last phase.”
Shane shook his head and took a deep sigh “Yes, Pinnacle Analytics got the jump on what we were doing there, I’m going to have settle my wager with Lucien now, such a smug little prick that one.”
Robb nodded “Yes Sir, we still don’t know how they got hold of the data to block our moves, but we’re working on it.”
“You probably won’t find it, Pinnacle is better at data hacking than anyone, but keep trying to see where we leaked though, would be good to plug the hole.” said Shane.
“Next project is number 23, as you recommended we’ve burned the project, the subject and associates have been terminated and all records expunged.” 
Shane nodded, “Good, I know it’s distasteful, but it was necessary. I want a review on all the testing we have for the subject and a reason why it went off the rails so badly by at our next meeting.”
Rob paled slightly and said “Yes sir” he picked up the file he’d placed on the coffee table at the start of the meeting “Now we get to project 29 Sir, lots of news on that one”
Shane sat forward and looked intently as Rob “Yes, I saw the latest bulletin, please explain how we’ve fucking lost him?”
Rob fumbled with the file momentarily, but quickly regained his composure “Sir, you’re correct that we did loose him, but it was for less than 48 hours, however he’s back in our sights now. I received and update just before I came in here that one of our scouts out near the Scar saw him and was able to get a tracker on him.”
Shane smiled slightly “That’s good news, get in contact with him and push the project forward as planned”
“Yes sir, already in motion”
Shane asked “Can we account for the missing 48 hours?”
“No Sir we can’t, we believe the Greys took him off the streets and moved him around until just recently.”
“Ok, there’s some work to here, we need to find out where he went and what he did with these Greys, it’s just not acceptable to have a subject missing for the best part of two days. Also I want to get us inside the Greys, the fact they are invisible to us is worrying. I need to know if any of the other Board companies are behind them.”
Shane stood up signalling an end to the meeting, Rob followed suit not missing the hint and retrieved all the files. They both walked over to the office door where Shane placed a hand on his shoulder in what anyone watching would have seen as a friendly gesture. He leaned in and said calmly and quietly, his hand still resting lightly on Rob’s shoulder “Rob, this project is feeling a lot like Project 23, I don’t want two projects crashing and burning so close to each other. Get control of this one or your position as Director of Special Projects might come to an end too, I don’t want to have to burn you along with it” His smile was warm and genuine despite the cold threat.
With that Shane patted Rob’s shoulder and gestured for him to leave, the meeting was over, Rob knew he was on borrowed time if he didn’t deliver.
#
Rob was back in his office on the 53rd floor of the Nova Corp headquarters, it was a thirty minute fast car drive from Shane’s palatial mansion in the hills to the north of the city where he actually ran Nova Corp. Rob didn’t have to worry about speeding, Nova took care of any tickets he might have been given, Shane made sure of that. 
Rob was sitting in his office thinking about the not so veiled threat Shane had dropped at the end of their meeting, he really needed to get Jim Dalton back on track and following the program Shane has mapped out for him years ago.
He pressed the call button on the phone on his desk “Grace, can you get me the special operations team in here asap.” without waiting for an answer he cut the line.
Minutes later three men were standing in his office, Like Rob they were all wearing expensive suits, but unlike Rob they didn’t look like they were born in the suits. Rather they looked like they resented wearing them and also resented being in the office.
Rob waited until the office door was shut behind them to address the men “Gentlemen, I’ve just been up the Hill to the big house and let me tell you the boss is not happy. We’ve got to get Dalton back on track now.”
He pointed at one of the men. “John, you need to find him and make contact, play it like you’re one of the Greys, we need him back on schedule and I need to know where he’s been for the last two days.”
The man simply nodded.
Rob pointed at the other two men “You two, when John gets some workable intel on the Greys, I need you to get inside and see who’s behind them. The boss wants to know if they are a front for any of the Board companies trying to fuck with us.”
One of the two men asked “If they’re not Board cut-outs, what then?”
“If they are actually independent, which I highly doubt, then we’ll turn them to work for us.”
Rob deliberately looked at each man in turn “I don’t need to tell you just how important it is we get results and get them fast. If we have another project fail we’ll all get burnt along with it, understood?”
All three men nodded at Rob “Ok, I want hourly updates, get on to it.”
With that the men left the office with out a backwards glance, Rob knew these men were absolutely professionals, if anyone could get them out of this mess, they could. Still he was worried, Jim Dalton had proved to me much smarter and resourceful than anyone anticipated and he’d already made Rob look stupid once, he wasn’t going to get a second chance.
#
In the hours that passed Rob had stayed in his office waiting for updates from his team. Getting the Dalton project back on track was quite literally the most important task he’d ever had, he needed laser focus to turn his fortunes around. 
His team on the ground were professionals and as instructed started dropping secured updates each hour, so far there was nothing of consequence, but Rob expected it would take some time to ramp up thier activity. 
In the meantime he needed to go back through all the data points to see what they’d missed about this subject Jim Dalton and how he was the second subject to get away from him in short order.
Nova Corp, through it’s various subsidiaries cast a wide net all the way from elementary and high school through to college and corporate testing to find candidates with the right mix of intelligence, independence, and resilience for thier special projects. Each time a citizen did any kind of test, Nova Corp scooped up the data, analysed it and used it for thier gain. At any one time they had a database of likely candidate for all kinds of jobs, actions and special projects.
Rob was looking through the raw data from Jim Dalton’s files to see if the appraisals team had made any errors or missed something. There was a knock at the door, it was Grace his executive assistant, she looked worried, had she picked up on the precarious position Rob was in? He wasn’t in the mood to discuss it one way or thew other?
“Grace, I don’t want to be disturbed today please” he told her.
“I know, Mr Hughes, but this is really important, I’ve just sent you a link to a video you need to watch” she said.
“I don;t have time for video’s today Grace, I’ll look at it later”
“Sorry Mr Hughes but it’s from Mr Dalton, it’s all over the news” 
Rob’s blood ran cold at the mention of Dalton, what new fuck-up had dropped in his lap from this project? He clicked on the link from Grace, it was from a well known gossip site, not the news. The sensational video title was “Dalton proclaims his innocence from unknown location”
Rob looked up and Grace was still standing there “Out” he yelled. He clicked the play button without bothering to see if Grace left or not.
The video opened on Jim sitting in a run down hotel room, the backdrop of peeling wall paint and the dim light making it look like Jim was back in a jail, he spoke.
“Hello, my name is Jim Dalton and… well I’m not sure what I want to say here… umm, first of all I’m not who the news makes me out to be, I’m no master criminal or anything like that. Sure I’m guilty of getting drunk and being rude to staff, which I know was wrong” the video must have been shot on a phone, for a second it went black as Jim inadvertently put his hand over the lense. 
The image cam back and Jim continued “Is that enough to ruin someones life over? I worked my way up from the Outer to Level 8 just like they say you can, but one mistake and it’s all gone. One bad day and the press are at your doorstep and the Police and the CEB are coming to arrest you. Is that fair?”
Jim looked around as if there was a sound off screen, not hearing anything further he spoke again “I’m not going to jail for something like that, did anyone ever ask who else was at lunch? Who invited me there and paid for all the wine? Who leaked the footage to the media? No the media just made me out to be some villain, just like you’ve seen before, I’m not, were they? I’m just a normal working man who is being made an example of so none of you ever step out line… to the media who hounded me out of my house, do your job and dig deeper.” The video went to black and ended.
Rob sat at his desk processing what he’d just seen, after a moment he yelled into the empty office “Fuck! Fuck! This fucking arsehole is going to get me killed” he slammed his hands down onto the desk so hard he felt pain shoot up his arms. This was the worst possible thing that could have happened. He needed to take stock.
The phone rang, it wasn’t routed through Grace’s desk as per normal, this was coming in direct, only one person could do that, Shane.
#
Rob broke out in a cold sweat, he couldn’t take the call, he just didn’t have an answer for Shane and he needed one desperately. Instead he almost bolted out of the office, as he rushed past Grace sitting at her desk and said “Tell him I’m in the bathroom”, Grace didn’t get the chance to answer as Rob was already gone.
He’d made his way to the executive bathroom, luckily it was empty, breathing a sight of relief he pulled out a mobile from his pocket and punched in a number, it was answered immediately “John, have you seen Dalton’s video?”
John without emotion “Yes Sir”
“And… any thoughts o how to play this?” Rob asked.
“It seems to me this could align well with our goals for the project, even speed up the timeline”
“Explain”
“Once I make contact, I’ll encourage more video’s, but I’ll steer him towards our goals and minimise any damage to us. We can build up a strong narrative to damage our competitors if we drop the right information into Dalton’s lap and encourage more videos”.
Rob was quiet and thought it over. Yes it could work, he’d never have thought of Dalton going public himself, he’d always planned for the media to do the work of using Dalton’s story to undermine the other Board Companies, but this could be much more powerful.
“Ok, do it, how close are you to making contact?” 
“Give me twenty-four hours, I’ve got a lead that I’m chasing down now”
“Ok keep me updated” Rob said and cut the line.
He looked at himself in the mirror, smoothed down his suit jacket and adjusted his tie, he was back in control, look like it he told himself.
Rob sauntered back to his office as if he didn’t have a worry in the world, he breezed past Grace without a word. He immediately dialled Shane’s direct number from his desk phone, Shane picked up in two rings, he didn’t speak.
Rob jumped straight in “Sorry Sir, I just stepped out of the office for a quick break, did you want an update?”
“Yes Rob, I want an update as to why Jim Dalton is now releasing videos onto the net?”
“That’s part of the project plan, I felt it would speed up our goals and be more authentic if Dalton was used directly instead of through media proxies” that was a stretch, but he’d put it out there now.
Silence for a moment, then “Rob, remind me again what the project goals are please?”
He wasn’t buying it though Rob, he needed to be careful this was a tightrope ”This was a phase one project, creating a sympathetic public figure and sow discord about the system.”
“And why are we doing that?”
Rob swallowed “I must admit Sir, I don’t have access to the full project goals, but I’m assuming we want to leverage this public pressure against one or more of the Board companies to our advantage.” He hoped he was correct.
“That’s sounds about right Rob, but bear in mind you don’t know what the end game is here, so before you go off on your own again, do me a favour and run it by me, ok?”
“Yes Sir.”
“This is a novel play with the videos, play it out for now, but know this, my finger is hovering over the burn button, so don’t let it get out of hand.” the line was disconnected.
Rob slumped back into his chair, he’d gone way out on a limb there, but it seemed to have worked. He now had some time to unscrew this mess, he buzzed Grace “Grace, can you get me our media director in here, tell him to cancel his plans for the day we’ve got some work to do.”


















CHAPTER 16

JIM AND NOVA (JIM 8)

Jim was sprawled on the sagging double bed in the small darkened hotel room that he was holed up in, half watching the News feed on the TV that was securely bolted to the wall. It hadn’t taken him long to find a hotel that would take his credit stick and ask no questions, he’d grown up in the Outer and knew where he needed to go, he didn’t want to go there, but he had no choice.
They called it the Reach and it wasn’t a place you went to unless you were desperate or degenerate. The Reach was a sprawling district on the far side of the Outer from the Scar, where the city butted up against the as yet unsettled mountains. It was a place where you could buy pretty much anything if you had the credits. The Metro Police mostly left it alone as long as the area as a whole stayed quiet and out of the news. It was a mixing pot of all levels, from those who had nowhere else to go, to the upper levels looking for some illicit thrills away from prying eyes.
It took the rest of the credits on one of his sticks to get a black market burner phone, the seller guaranteed it was untraceable, but Jim wasn’t taking any chances  and the phone remained powered off until he needed it again.
He knew it was a desperate move to upload the video, Jim reasoned that there was enough interest in his story the news might pick it up and just maybe his side of the story would get out there. So far nothing had hit the news yet.
He lay still on the bed, barely moving, physically and mentally exhausted, the weight of his current situation pressing him further into the sagging mattress.
 His last hope, the Greys turned out to be just another group of people who wanted to use him for thier own ends, he was done with that, from now on he resolved he’d only deal on his own terms. If he crashed and burned, he’d make them all hurt on the way down.
The flashing red alert banner on the TV caught Jim’s attention and roused him out of his dozing. He sat up and listened intently, the anchor was clearly excited at this news “We have a breaking news story, former Level 8 executive and wanted fugitive Jim Dalton has uploaded a video proclaiming his innocence from charges of assault, criminal damage, theft and arson from his three day crime spree and also denying any connection to dissident group the Greys. We’ll have a deep dive into the video and Dalton’s claims at the top of the hour, be sure to join us then” the channel moved back to it’s current show.
Jim lay back on the bed pleased his gamble had paid off, perhaps the tide of public opinion was going to turn for him. Looking at the clock on the TV he had about twenty minutes, plenty of time to get some supplies before it would be too hard to walk around in public safely.
#
John was waiting in a small ten seat hole in the wall cafe for a call, he knew Dalton would surface sooner or later and he’d bet it would be in the Reach, everyone who wanted to hide ended up in the Reach eventually. 
John had access to Dalton’s whole life story from Nova, he knew how he thought, how he’d react under pressure, how far he’d go to save himself, or so he’d thought. Dalton had surprised them all with the video, that kind of resourcefulness wasn’t in the files. John knew full well the files weren’t everything, under intense pressure people could do unexpected things, the could learn and adapt. If his boss ever got out into the field and did the kind of work John did for him, he might have understood this.
Not that he didn’t respect Rob Hughes, any Level 8 who had the ear of the CEO of one of the Board companies was a full measure of respect, but they lived a life disconnected from the people out here in the Outer. Even if the did sneak into the Reach for some debauchery, people like John were always waiting in the shadows to save them from themselves.
He’d worked on the Dalton project from it’s very beginning and had built up a begrudging respect for the man, they put him though hell and he hadn’t crumbled yet. Still that wouldn’t stop Rob from using Dalton as a pawn to displace Rob and take his seat, a seat he knew he do a better job sitting in.
His phone rang, he clicked the earpiece and said “Speak to me”, after a few seconds he nodded and ended the call.
He took a sip of his coffee and grimaced, it wasn’t very good, there was no real coffee out here just a cheap synthetic brew that sort of approximated coffee, sometimes. He flicked through his contacts on his phone and dialled Rob.
Rob must have been waiting with his phone in his hand as he answered halfway through the first ring “Have you got news John?” he demanded.
John answered calmly “Yes Sir, one of my spotters tell me he’s holed up in a hotel in the Reach, I’m not far from there, I should have eyes on soon”
“Good work John, what’s the play when you make contact?”
“Right now I’m not sure, I’ll need some time to see where his head is at before I make a move, first contact is always a fluid situation, I can’t sell him straight away.” said John, he never prejudged how a first contact went, people had strong bullshit meter and if you were pushing an agenda you’d get found out most of the time.
“Ok, understand this, we don’t have a lot of time here, the video is going mainstream and we need to get in front on this now” Rob was getting agitated again.
John answered simply “Understood Sir, I’ll update once contact is made” and killed the call. 
He sat at the rickety table and looked at the now cold fake coffee in front of him, Rob was going to be a problem he thought. 
He got up and left the cafe, he was only about ten blocks away from the hotel Dalton was holed up in, the walk would help get him into the mood of the Reach and free his mind from the corporate pressures, a sort of pre-contact meditation by soaking in his surroundings. John was enjoying this already.
#
Jim pulled his cap down and kept his chin down as he walked through the small hotel lobby and past the receptionist who didn’t look up as he was too busy slurping on a bowl of instant noodles. That reminded Jim how hungry he was, he didn’t remember being so hungry so often, Jim figured the stress of being on the run was energy sapping work as walked onto the street. 
He’d scouted a corner store a block over before he checked in and he headed purposefully across the street towards it, he held his head low avoiding eye contact with anyone on the street, he was trying to look like any other down on thier luck resident of the Reach. 
With his head own, Jim didn’t see the man detach himself from the crowd milling around a food stall a few buildings down from the hotel, and he did see the man following Jim’s path to the corner store.
Jim slinked into the corner store and grabbed a basket, he’d need to stock for a least a week, once he was back a the hotel room he wasn’t coming out again.
The hotel only had a grimy old microwave and at kettle, so Jim was picking out whatever he could that would work with the meagre facilities in the room. He was standing in the aisle looking at the ready made meals, a wall of VatSoy in various flavours, all claiming to taste like various different meats, with images of the real thing on the packaging. 
Jim wasn’t fooled but the advertising but he had no choice and started picking out some that sounded like they’d be ok when a stranger approached him. The man was obviously fresh out one of the twenty-four hour clubs that the Reach was famous for, and clearly still under the influence of whatever he’d been partying with, he sidled up to Jim and said “Oh yeah, got the hungers myself my friend!”
Jim smiled and nodded, trying to avoid opening a conversation with the stranger.
The man persisted “Oh… love the Chicken Curry flavour, make sure you grab one of these…” he lost track of his sentence as he grabbed a couple of the chicken curry VatSoy packets.
He turned to Jim and held out the packets he’d just taken off the shelf “Hey man, you wouldn’t be able to shout me these two would you? I’m kind of short right now.”
Fuck, thought Jim, this was the last thing he needed. “Sorry I can’t, I’ve only got enough for my food” Jim kept his head down.
“Man come on, it’s just two packets, only like 20 credits, I’m good for it” He looked was looking directly at Jim who was not returning his gaze. A flicker of recognition crossed the mans face.
“Hey, do I know you?”
Jim’s heart was in his mouth “No, we’ve never met, don’t worry about it“ He turned to walk away, but the stranger gabbed him.
“Wait, I know who you are , you’re on the news… aren’t you that guys that the Police are looking for, the one who blew up the car and beat up some reporters?”
Jim tried to shake off the man’s grip, but he held on tight. “No that’s not me, why don’t you just grab whatever you want an I’ll pay for it.”
“Fuck that shit man, do you know how many points I’ll get for turning you in, you’re not going anywhere.” The stranger had sobered up completely now and his grip on Jim tightened. 
“Don’t make a fucking sound, I’m not sharing my reward money with anyone” He pulled his phone out of his pocket to call the Police.
