 Another stranger stepped into the aisle and closed the distance to Jim and his assailant quietly and efficiently, he jabbed something into the mand back and he went limp, dropping to the floor with a thud, out cold.
“We need to get out of here right now”
Jim paused “Who the fuck are you?”
“Call me John, if you don’t want to get arrested, come with me now” as he said that he bent down to take the man’s wallet and phone from the limp body. Jim registered the situation he was in, his cover almost blown and a body at his feet, he moved.
 John lead him out of the shop, stopping at the counter to tell the cashier “There’s a drunk passed out in the aisle back there, better get the Police to come and collect him.” The cashier just rolled his eyes and started dialling.
John stopped on the footpath and asked Jim “You need to get back out of sight, have you got a place to go?”
Jim simply said “Thanks for you help John, I’m good now” and started to walk away.
This was the time for John to take a gamble “Jim, you need somewhere safer than a pay by the hour hotel, especially since you video is about to go mainstream.”
Jim stopped dead in the track hearing his name and mention of his video, he turned on John “Who the hell are you? how do you know my name?”
“Relax, I’m a friendly, I’m just here to keep you safe”
“Did Vex send you to find me?”
Who was Vex thought John, it must be one of the Grey’s, time for another gamble. “No, Vex didn’t send me, I’m got my job from someone else, but we can talk about that later, let’s not stand on the street. The hotel is compromised, we can’t go back there, I’ve got a place we can hole up in for a bit so you can work our your next move safely.”
Jim didn’t want to go with anyone, anywhere, but he now had new questions. If John wasn’t working for Vex, was there another group of Grey’s working against her? Right now he needed to get off the street and somewhere safe. 
“Ok, lets go” he said.
#
They walked past the hotel on the other side of the street, Jim didn’t have anything in there he needed anyway, the reality was he didn’t actually own anything anymore apart from the second hand clothes he was wearing.
John walked briskly and ducked down side alleys a few times stopping to survey the crowd covertly to see if anyone else had picked up Jim’s tail. The streets of the Reach were always busy any time of day or night, so the chance of being seen grew stronger with each passing step out on the street. Jim felt exposed, and wasn’t sure how much longer he could walk around, the incident at the corner shop replaying in his mind, at any time someone else could grab him just like the drunk had. 
If he was being honest he wasn’t really that comfortable following John, was he lying about not knowing Vex, was this just a ruse for her to exact revenge for running from her? Either way Jim was entirely unhappy with his situation.
John pulled them into another side alley, this time he didn’t stop to look up and down the street, instead he walked down the alley and disappeared around a corner. Jim rushed to catch up and saw John standing at an open door in the back of the building.
“Hurry up I want this door closed” said John,
Jim scooted past him into the building, he immediately saw that the door opened directly onto a set of stairs that went down into a basement. Jim Paused as John closed the door behind him with a heavy thud and punched in a code on the discreet keypad on the wall. 
Jim a few steps down hadn’t seen the keypad when he rushed in, suspiciously looked at it. John saw the look “Don’t worry Jim, just disabling the security system, the door doesn’t lock from this side, you can get out anytime you want.” He brushed past Jim and went down the stairs.
At the bottom of the stairs there was a small landing and another door, this one was just like a normal door, not the steel door to they just come through from the outside, John opened it and stepped inside without any fanfare.
Cautiously Jim followed John inside to the basement, looking around he could see this was nothing like the sparse second-hand set-up of the safe room out by the Scar that Vex had wanted him to stay in. 
The contrast couldn’t have been more stark, the main room they’d stepped into was well furnished with what looked like newly bought furniture, there was a set of large matching lounges around a large wall screen. The kitchen was large and well appointed almost professional, from where he was standing he could see bedrooms off to the side, in fact it looked a lot like a high end apartment, just without the windows.
John spoke up break Jim’s surveying “Ok, a quick tour and then I think you need a shower and some new clothes.”
Jim looked down at himself in the second hand clothes he’d been wearing for days now, he couldn’t disagree with that.
John continued and waved around the room they we’re standing in “Main lounge and kitchen, obviously, over there a couple of bedrooms, ensuite for each.” He moved towards a door that was on the opposite side of the room and opened the door.
“…and here this is the office I guess you’d call it” the room was stacked with computer racking along one wall and a commercial grade looking terminal on the other. Jim couldn’t help himself and whistled at the sight of the tech humming and blinking with tiny lights.
“Yep, its a pretty solid set-up, we can tap into just about anything, that’s kind of what we do down here. Do me a favour and don’t mess with anything in here, it’s not cheap and we can’t just go out and replace this stuff.”
Jim nodded and asked “Is that terminal connected to the grid?”
Almost boasting now, John answered “Sure is, but it’s kind of a one way system, it won’t allow inbound comms, like you can search around for anything you want, but it’ll cut you off if it thinks something is trying to reach back to you”
John flicked off the light and closed the door.
This place seemed too good to be true “So who are you really?” Jim asked.
“Like I said, we have mutual friends, but I’m not looking for you to do anything for me, I just want to keep you safe until you work out your next move and perhaps help if I can”
“So you know Vex?”
John, waved him off “No I don’t, but we don’t all know each other, that’s kind of the point though. Look I’ll answer more questions later, I’ve got a couple of urgent things to do right now that you’re here, not least of which is grab some supplies, the kitchen is empty and I wasn’t expecting guests, I’ll be back in a couple of hours.”
Jim didn’t think he’d really answered the questions, but he wasn’t forcing anything onto him right now, which was a welcome change. He decided that’d he’d take the comfort of a safe place for the night, a warm shower and a real bed.
John was halfway out the door and Jim called to him “You mentioned a code to get in?”
“That’s right I did” he reached into his pocked and pulled out a small plastic card and flipped it across the room the Jim, who caught expert throw easily
“That’s a prox card, it will open the door and disarm the alarm, just wave it on the door, but if I were you I’d be staying put for a while, it won’t take long for the incident a the store to hit mainstream and then everyone will be out to find you. Catch you later”
He closed the door to the stairs and left, Jim was alone again in someone else’s safe house. This was so different to the Grey’s he’d encountered before, clearly well funded and not the least bit afraid of technology like Vex and her crew. Was this a rich offshoot, or something altogether different, he’d find out one way of the other, but for now, a hot shower.
#
Once John had left the building he made a call.
“Speak” Rob was on the line immediately
“I’ve got him” said matter of factly.
“Where”
“He’s in one of our data siphoning safe houses out in the Reach, I just left him there.”
“Good, make sure you keep him under lock and key, we can’t afford to lose him”
“Yes Sir, he’s tucked up nice and tight.”
Rob audibly sighed on the other end of the line “Ok, I’ll come back to you with a plan of attack in twelve hours.”
“Roger that Sir” the line was cut.
John shook his head, he knew what he needed to do, Rob didn’t realise he had the man, so he had control now.
He sent a text to a different number
[Project 29 is in location DS354 under my control]
The response was instant.
[Copy, proceed with variation Delta]
John smiled as he walked into the street.

















CHAPTER 17

NEIL’S NEW JOB (NEIL 7)

Vex was pacing the room, silent with fury; no one dared to speak. The main room of the Grey’s safe house out in the scar territory was full this morning. Vex had demanded an unprecedented meeting of all of the Grey’s cell leaders. Normally a meeting like this was far too risky, but today Vex wanted answers and wasn’t about to waste time going through the normal slow analogue communications channels.
Vex stopped pacing and spoke “How the fuck does a corporate executive, who’s been sitting behind desk and sipping wine for twenty years, suddenly bust out of this safe house and then just disappear?”
No one answered.
Vex pointed at Corra “Just tell me how you fucked up so bad?”
Corra paled. “He caught me off guard, I didn’t think for one moment he would run”
“When did I ever tell you we made assumptions like that here? We can’t afford to. Now there’s a man out that in the city who knows my face, and can identify half the room here. Worse still he knows about this safe house. We don’t know where he is, or what he’ll do; if he gets caught by the CEB all of what we’ve worked for these last few year will be blown.” 
Again the room was silent; it was best not to give Vex a target when she was angry.
Vex started pacing again “Brask, you’re up; Rook is blown with Jim. I need you to find Jim, we can’t have him out there in the wild. I need you to find him fast, you just know the CEB is gunning for him too.”
Brask nodded “Got it, and when I find him?” the question hung heavy in the silence of the room.
Vex took a moment then answered “Bring him to me, I’ll talk to him. If he’s not on board with us; he can rot under the swamp in the Scar.”
Brask nodded again and got up to leave, he didn’t want to stay around for what was coming next. 
Vex watched him leave, deliberately not speaking until she knew he’d left the safe house. She turned to speak to Rook next; he was sitting at the kitchen table drinking a cooling synth coffee.
“Rook, you’re not dropping Jim completely; Brask has got the lead on him for now, but keep an eye out in case we can’t grab him and we have to go more, kinetic. You can go”, She dismissed him.
Again Vex waited until Rook left before continuing. She spoke to a man who was sitting in the shadows at the back of the room. She spoke with a level of respect no one had been afforded at the meeting so far.
“You need to get our new friend onboard with us quickly. We’re going to need access into Nova’s systems sooner rather than later.” Vex Said
The man answered “Are you sure? Right now he doesn’t know who I am. Besides, he’s no hacker.”
“I know but he’s got access to terminals that can get us what we need.”
“That was just a simple task, a mere theft, what you’re talking about is a serious hack.”
“I know, but with fucking Jim Dalton possibly blowing this whole show any time, I don’t think we have any choice but to bring out plans forward.”
The man considered her statement “No, I don’t think we do” he paused and Vex waited for him to speak again “What about this place?” he asked.
Vex looked around at Corra and the three other people were left in the room. She raised her eyebrow to the man who quietly nodded.
Vex barked to the others in the room. “Ok, we’ve got to assume this place is burnt; pack it all up and make sure there’s nothing left but ashes when you’re done. Corra you and I can start downstairs.”
With that vex walked across the room and down the stairs, Corra followed happy to have been spared any more public dressing down.
The three people left in the room looked to the man still sitting in the back of the room. “You heard her, let’s not waste time”
They silently moved to different corners of the room and started unpacking cupboards and drawers; this was clearly something they’d practiced. 
A single gunshot rang out from downstairs; they all stopped dead in thier tracks. 
The man stood at the back of the room stood up and walked towards the stairs just as Vex emerged silently. He looked at her, an expression of genuine concern on his face, much like a father might look upon a daughter who’s had to do a difficult thing. They didn’t speak, an unspoken question and answer seemed to pass between them. Vex broke his gaze and looked passed him and saw everyone staring at them. 
She barked “Get on with it, we’ve got fifteen minutes before we’re all out of here.”
#
It was early in the morning and Neil was awake, much to his annoyance. It was too early to be be awake; he’d been struggling to sleep ever since he’d seen his wife and daughter and found out they’d all been deceived. He racked his brain, but just couldn’t put his finger on who or why would do that to him; it kept him up most nights trying to work the problem. If he was being honest with himself he was also nervous with anticipation about the next job the Grey’s gave him. The last job was equally terrifying and thrilling; but he had to admit it was the most alive he’d felt in years.
The street lights outside were seeping their weak yellow light through the thin curtains into the bedroom of his meagre apartment in the halfway house. The sun wasn’t ready yet to light the sky. 
Neil stumbled into the small living area, not bothering to turn on the room’s lights. He liked the darkness, it soothed his soul after five years in prison where the lights never went out. He checked the wall clock. It was about thirty minutes before his alarm was set to go off. 
“Every fucking morning” thought Neil
He flopped down and sprawled across the small two-seat lounge, its worn springs creaking and flicked on the TV bolted to the wall. The news of the day was playing with another update on Jim Dalton.
“ …Dalton hasn’t been seen now for a least 4 days; despite hundreds of claimed sightings ranging from the Scar all the way across the to Reach. Metro Police remain concerned about his safety and are actively pursuing all leads. No words on the group called the Grey’s; they remain largely unknown. Metro Police are continuing to look into who is behind the group, but have not been able to pin the group down as yet…”
Neil flicked the mute button… the TV’s hum died, leaving only old news. He’d been following Jim Dalton’s case closely, ever since the Greys were tied to his downfall. He felt an odd connection to Dalton’s story. If he was honest, he felt a bit special knowing some of the Greys directly and having worked for them. ‘Did that make him a Grey?’ he wondered.
A quiet knock sounded at the door. Neil stared at it, wishing he could see through to whoever was there so early in the morning. Probably Grant, that nosy bastard, poking around. He really didn’t like Grant… sometimes it felt like he was still in jail.
Reluctantly he got up off the couch, stretched and sauntered over to the door, he was in no rush. Whoever it was could wait. He stopped at the door and hesitated, the building was silent, did he imagine the knock?
The knock came again, just as quietly as before. He opened the door, it was Knox.
Neil was shocked, what was Knox doing here? He hadn’t spoken to him for weeks; ever since he’d got settled into his job he’d had no need for Knox’s advice. Seeing Knox standing there, he immediately felt guilty for dropping him like that. 
“Knox, what are you doing here, and so early?”
Knox smiled “Are you going to let me in?” he countered.
“Of course, come in. It’s not much, buts it’s mine for only half my monthly allowance” Neil said dripping with sarcasm.
Knox chuckled as he moved inside “Yep, things haven’t changed much since I got out, my first place was pretty much the same.”
 “How long ago was that now?”
Knox sat at the small table “Must be at least ten years ago now.”
“Ten years, and still grinding it out with the rest of us our here, seems pretty rigged to me”
Knox nodded in agreement, and asked “You wouldn’t be able to make me a coffee, I had an early start and something makes me think this will be a long day.”
Neil moved over the small kitchenette and pulled out a jar of instant coffee. It was the best he could find in the loca area; he’d splurged a fair chunk of his left over credits for it. He’d figured if he had to live in this shit hole at least he could look forward a half decent coffee each morning.
Waiting for the kettle to boil he asked “Knox, its nice to see you, what the hell are you doing here at my place so damn early. Is it about my job?” Neil was starting to get worried that his escapade of a few days ago had been discovered.
“No, it’s not about your day job, but it is about a job of sorts”
“Knox you’re not making any sense.” Neil was very confused now.
The kettle boiled and Neil filled two cups with hot water, the smell that wafted up was almost like real coffee. Knox waited silently for Neil to sit at the table; he sipped at the steaming black coffee with his eyes closed savouring the warmth of the black liquid.
“I see you haven’t skimped on this, it’s the good stuff.” Knox sat forward and fixed his eyes onto Neil. this wasn’t the kind hearted man Neil thought he knew; the gaze was suddenly all business.
“Neil, remember when I told you to steer clear of Rook and his type?”
 “Yes” Neil said simply, he didn’t expect the conversation to go in this direction; he wasn’t sure he liked it.
“Well, it seems like you didn’t listen to me and now they want you to do some more things for them.”
Neil was shocked “Wait, are you involved with them; did they send you here?”
Knox’s gaze didn’t move, after a pause he simply said “Yes, and yes.”
“Fucking hell Knox, are you telling me you were one them from the start?”
“Neil, under normal circumstances you’d never have known. This Jim Dalton that’s been all over the news has meant a change of plans; an acceleration if you will.”
Neil was floored, Knox the man who’d helped him out as a mentor showing him the ropes when he first started working was a Grey the whole time.
“Knox, I don’t know what to say.”
Knox smiled a warm disarming smile that made him look like a regular kind old man again and said “You don’t have to say anything Neil, you have to do something. I believe last time you and Vex spoke you said you were ready for more work. We’ll, I’m here to give you your next job. Are you ready?”
This was not how he thought this morning would go, but it was what he‘d been waiting for. The Greys had surprised him yet again; Neil was seriously impressed with how well they could keep doing that. These were people who could get stuff done and he was all for it.
“I’m ready.” he said; he was absolutely certain he was.
#
An hour or so later Neil was walking out of the lift through the little ground floor lobby of the halfway house. Just like every other morning, six days a week, he was on his way to catch the bus to his job out in the Rust Belt. This morning Grant was waiting at the door to his office and called over to Neil.
“Neil, let’s chat for a moment.” He asked.
“Can we chat after work? I don’t want to miss my bus” said Neil.
“Don’t worry about that bus, it’s not yours anymore” He gestured for Neil to follow him into the office.
Neil was immediately on guard, his pulse quickening… had Grant heard something about his work at Stratum Projects? Had he been caught stealing the files? Had the Security Director reported him after all… or was it his late-night dealings with the Greys? Grant had already warned him to keep his nose clean. He didn’t want a final warning.
Neil followed him into the office. Grant didn’t sit and didn’t extend the invitation for Neil to sit either.
“I don’t know how; but you’ve been reassigned to a new job starting today.”
Neil was as shocked as Grant.
“Reassigned, to where?” Neil blurted out.
“Well, as of about an hour ago you’re moving to a new job with a company called… Nova Corporation.” Grant swivelled his terminal screen around to show Neil the message. Before Neil had a chance to read it, Grant continued “I’m all my time here managing this place, I’ve never seen a message like this; it’s real, I’ve checked.”
Neil was unsure of what to say, so instead awkwardly shrugged.
“I mean, it’s not even in the Rust Belt, apparently some sort of data centre close to the city.”
The penny dropped… Neil’s gut twisted. This was Knox’s new job, apparently. It was the place he’d stolen the plans for. Neil hadn’t expected a real job when Knox mentioned it. He’d braced for another clandestine task… yet the Greys had blindsided him again.
He had to be careful not to tip off Grant that he now understood what was going on. It was all too easy for Neil to play this off as a surprise “How? Did I do something wrong”
“Don’t be a smart arse, I’m sure you know exactly what’s going on.” Grant shot back, his surprise replaced by irritation.
Neil stared at Grant. “Look, I don’t know what you’ve got yourself mixed up with; but my advice is: get yourself out of it. This won’t end well.” said Grant
Neil didn’t respond again, he was getting annoyed at Grant now. The man was part of the system, a bottom feeder for sure; but he was a part of the system keeping Neil down. Neil was taking real action now and this man was not just standing in his way; he was getting upset that Neil now had connections.
“I don’t know what you’re talking about, I’m just doing my time here like everyone else. If I was doing anything wrong you’d report me.” Neil said as nonchalantly as he could.
Grant shook his head in disgust “You and I both know I’m not reporting you for anything. Your friends have made it very clear what would happen if I did.”
Neil didn’t know that, but he wasn’t surprised. He found himself quite pleased to hear Grant had been warned off. The power had clearly shifted here and he leant into it.
“That’s right, my friends and I wouldn’t be happy if you did that.”
Growing more confident Neil pressed his new found power “Now tell me where I need to go and we can stop pretending you’ve got any say in what I do from now on.”
Grant was silent for a moment, Neil could see the anger in his face. Eventually he spat out “Details are on your phone, now get the fuck out of my office.”
Neil didn’t need to be asked twice, this was turning out to be a good day.
#
Neil watched the bus he used to take each morning pull away from the curb, the eyes of his former fellow passengers staring out of the windows at him full of questions. He shook off the feeling he’d never occupy the same space as those people again. He’d read the instructions on his phone, his new bus was leaving from a few blocks over the walk would give him time to take stock of the morning.
He was coming to realise that he’d been consistently underestimating the Greys. It seemed with every passing day they demonstrated just how wide, and fast, they could reach out and exert influence. 
What really shocked Neil was the revelation that Knox was connected to the Greys. The seemingly gentle and helpful old Knox who’d shown him the ropes in his first few weeks out in the Rush Belt and helped him get settled into the halfway house. The Knox who was everyone’s friend, was a Grey; associated with Rook, Brask and Vex. What made Neil pause for thought the most was that Knox had actually warned him off dealing with the Grey’s when they first met; just how long had Knox and the Greys been working him?
He immediately shook off the thought; for now his burning need get retribution on those who’d engineered his downfall and had taken away his family, outweighed any reservations about being manipulated by the Greys. Not forgotten Neil thought, just put aside as long as thier goals remained the same.
Neil was actually buzzing with anticipation at the prospect of working at Nova Corp. It was the first time in years he was generally excited about something new. Still, there was a growing nervousness about what they were going to ask him to do. He’d only just survived the last task they’d assigned him, and that was in no small part down to dumb luck. 
Lost in thought, he’d already arrived at the bus stop he’d been allocated, it was empty. No crowd of sleepy workers ready for thier next twelve hour day waiting for thier bus like he was used to. He checked again the instructions on his phone, did he have the right place? The street signs said he was in the right spot; Neil stood on the curb looking up and down the street for the bus. 
A white van pulled up and the side door opened directly in front of Neil. He could see into the windows that this van had been converted to carry about nine or so passengers. There was no driver to welcome him; at that moment his phone pinged. He looked at it and the message that arrived simply said 
[Transport has arrived, please alight vehicle]
Neil did as it asked and sat down in the second row of the van. The door started to shut and them shuddered to a stop, the lights in the van flickered.
Neil looked around his sense of danger rising, did he need to get out he thought? He was bracing himself to flee the van.
Brask, from out of nowhere jumped in; Neil jumped back in surprise.
“Stay put” Brask barked at Neil as he flipped a switch on the blocker he was holding in his hand, this one had three buttons unlike the single on-off button Neil’s unit had.
The lights immediately stopped flickering and the door resumed it’s path, closing smoothly as muffling the street noise. 
Brask sat across the aisle from Neil, he spoke as the van pulled noiselessly away from the curb. “I don’t have much time, we’ve got about five minutes before the van alerts back to base there is a problem with the system.”
He held up the blocker and showed it to Neil “This unit’s blocking all out going signals and can stall the whole the whole system, but it’s got it limits, so listen carefully”
Neil understood and simply said “Got it, what have you got for me?”
“Ok, the plans you stole were for Nova Corp’s new data centre, we managed to get you in as a low level systems installer…”
Neil interrupted “Systems installer, I don’t know anything about systems..”
Brask cut him off “…this is not a two way conversation, shut the fuck up and listen.” He stared at Neil who didn’t respond.
“Ok, you know enough about plans and building to bluff your way through, we’ll only need you in there for a short time anyway.”
Brask paused and checked the screen on the console, it was still showing a “no network available” warning. 
Satisfied, he continued. “This new data centre they’re building is going to be the most advanced system built since before the Reclamation. We think most of the citizenship points management is going to end up running on it, so we want to get a backdoor into the system. It’s not fully secure yet so you need to find a weak point that we can drop our code into.”
“What am I looking for exactly?” Neil asked.
“We need to install hidden transmitter directly into one of the servers. We’ve got a team working on it now, so don’t ask me any questions about it.”
“I wouldn’t have a clue how to do that” Neil protested.
“I don’t care, apparently others think you’ll be able to work it. All I know is you’ve only got a few days to sniff around and find out how and where we can get our device plugged in. After that the system will be fully functional and will find anything we install, our window is small and getting smaller.”
Neil took a deep breath and nodded “Ok, I’ve got it”
Brask seemed to accept his answer. He flipped a switch again on his blocker and the van’s lights flickered again. The van shuddered to a stop, it felt like the Van was fighting against some unseen hand trying to stop it. The door popped open and Brask pushed his way out. “Knox will swing by your apartment for updates at seven tonight.”
He got out and skilfully blended into the streetscape.
The van came back to life; chimes sounded in the cabin as the system alerted Neil to the door being open. The centre console flashed back into life, the network was found and the Nova Corp logo smoothly rotated on the screen. The van pulled back into to traffic as if nothing had happened and continued on it’s way to it’s destination.
Neil sat back and let it take him to his next big task. He could do this; he must do this he though to himself. If there was a chance to hack or even break the system, he was all in.












CHAPTER 18

JIM’S VIDEO (JIM 9)

Jim woke up slowly, savouring the comfort and warmth of the bed. The silence and feeling of safety being tucked away in this luxury bunker was like a second blanket wrapped around him. He felt truly rested for the first time since the whole nightmare had began. 
He didn’t know how long he slept, or what time it was; there were no windows in the bedroom. The only light in the room was softly leaking under the door from the main lounge and kitchen area outside the bedroom. 
He stretched and rolled around luxuriating in the comfort of the queen sized bed, a real actual bed, not some bunk or old couch. Jim never thought such simple pleasures of a warm bed and a full stomach could be so good.
He wasn’t ready to get up yet. There was no need to, no alarms scheduled, no places he needed to go. In fact lying there basking in his immediate comfort, Jim could picture himself holed up in this basement for ever. 
Jim knew it was just a dream though. His eyes still closed unwilling to break the spell, he reflected on this latest turn of events. He was, for the time being, safely squirrelled away here. John had brought in enough supplies last night for at least a week, along with a fresh hot take-away meal from one of the many hole in the wall restaurants plying thier trade all night in the Reach.
He thought more about John, although he doubted that was him name. The man had stepped in at the last minute to rescue him, what a coincidence that John just happened to be in the store at the right time and had this basement hide-out nearby all ready to go. Jim didn’t think it was just luck that it all lined up for him so neatly, he filed that question to the growing list of things he didn’t understand.
Jim assumed John was connected to the Greys, although he had never really confirmed it. That was how they operated Jim thought; never directly answering anything. It was a tactic Jim was going to use more often now too.
He thought about what he could confirm; John didnt know Vex, which might be a good thing for Jim considering how he left Vex’s hospitality. Jim could see that this branch of the Greys, was very different to Vex’s group. The seemed to have a lot more resources and were definitely not scared of technology, the commercial grade server set-up in this basement was evidence of that. 
He wondered how many other factions the Greys were out there? Discovering that this world of underground resistance existed was both shocking and liberating at the same time.
One thing Jim did know was that John had shown him more respect and friendliness than any of the Vex’s Greys had, and he was going to soak up that hospitality until he knew what he was going to do next.
What was he going to do next he thought? He didn’t actually know, he’d been so busy surviving that thinking long-term had not even been on his radar, all he’d done was react and run. 
Today he was going to figure out a way to fight back, but first he was going to cook himself a big breakfast and brew a big hot mug of coffee or two. He opened his eyes, threw off the bedsheets and sat-up, invigorated that he was finally ready to take control by what lay ahead. 
#
Jim was sitting at the small table in the kitchenette of the bunker finishing up his big breakfast with great satisfaction. He looked over at the mess he’s made when he cooked it and supposed he should clean up after himself, after all he was a guest, possibly a prisoner, the line between those two ideas was getting blurry in his life at the moment. 
A low beeping sound started coming from the server room across from where he was sitting. John had shown him the room when they came in yesterday, he’s was too tired to take much in at the time, but it was certainly nothing like the clapped out techno-phobic safe house out in the Scar territory. 
The beeping was persistent, Jim still chewing a last mouthful of toast walked over to the server roon door went to opened, fully expecting it to be locked. It wasn’t and it opened silently into the room. Jim stopped at the door impressed for the second time by the sheer value of the equipment in the room. The room itself was as big as the whole open plan lounge kitchen he just ate breakfast in. One whole side of the room was taken up by racks of high end servers, it looked like at least half a dozen of them, all producing a low hum from the whirring fans, blue red and green lights were blinking across all of them. These servers were not sitting idle.
The beeping was coming from one of the two workstations on the other side of the room. Each terminal was set-up on dark grey steel desks of like the ones you’d repair electronics at rather than office desks. They held only a mouse, keyboard and large LCD screen.
One of the screens was responsible for the beeping and Jim walked over to it hesitantly. The screen display a simple message line in white text on the otherwise black screen, there seems to be no operation system working at all.
[New message received – Do you want to view Y/N]
Jim didn’t actually want to interfere with the terminal, he had no idea what they were doing in here and wasn’t about to mess it up for them and potentially put himself in more danger.
Just as he was surveying the room, the terminal beeped again this time it felt more more insistent, he looked at the screen.
[New message received (2) – Do you want to view Y/N]
Someone clearly wanted to talk to John, or whoever was running this site, Jim was tempted to answer and see what was going on.
[Answer Y now Jim]
Jim stood there staring at the screen, the message didn’t make any sense, no-one knew he was there, unless it was John who wanted to talk to Jim. That made sense and it might be important so Jim sat at the terminal and press the Y key.
A chat window opened up immediately.
[Hello Jim]
Jim typed “Who is this, is it you John? What’s going on?”
[No it’s not John, I’m a colleague of his, we’re working on your case together]
That didn’t sound very Grey like thought Jim.
“Who are you?” asked typed again.
[I can’t tell you at the moment, but I have information you need to know]
“How did you know I’m here?” Jim typed getting nervous, perhaps he wasn’t as safe as he thought.
[Jim, you don’t think we have surveillance in every room there? I waited for you finish your breakfast before messaging you]
Jim whipped his head around to look at the room, sure enough there was a small black lens on the ceiling in one of the corners of the room, you had to look to see it, but it wasn’t hidden, just small. He got up and bolted out of the room into the lounge and within seconds found two more black lenses in the ceiling. He couldn’t believe he’d missed them, in fact it was worse, he hadn’t even thought to look.
Shit! Jim thought, his illusion of safety shattered, the hearty breakfast now sitting heavy in his gut. They’d been watching him the whole time like he was an exhibit in a zoo.
Jim had and overwhelming urge to get out and just run, he raced up the stairs to the door to the outside and pushed. Nothing, the door didn’t move at all, the little keypad on the wall stubbornly displayed a red light. John had lied to him, he was not set-up to come and go as he pleased. 
Out of frustration Jim hammered on the door until his hands hurt. He slumped down against the door and closed his eyes, he was trapped again and they was nothing he could do about it.
#
Jim wasn’t sure how long he’d been sitting at the top of the stairs just staring into space. What he did know was that his back was starting to hurt and leaning against the steel door was making him cold, the stairs were not comfortable place to sit and contemplate. This latest setback, being trapped here in the luxury basement, had affected him more than he expected. Perhaps it was the brief hours where he felt he finally taken control of his situations and was using John and this bunker for his own plans only to have it snatched away in an instant. Jim’s willpower and resolve clearly had a limit, and he’d reached it.
Reluctantly he stood up, his body creaked and cracked, he was really feeling the fall-out of weeks on the run. Jim slunk down the stairs, not actually wanting to go back into the relative luxury of the bunker, it was embarrassing to have been tricked again.
Thankfully the beeping has stopped in the server room, Jim ignored the open door and decided he need a strong coffee to help gather his thoughts. He walked over to the kitchen and turned the high-end coffee machine on. It started doing its thing, heating, pumping, grinding and making all sorts of noise to prepare a fresh coffee for Jim. He stood there silently, the noises an oddly soothing distraction from the dull silence of the room. 
The beeping started again. 
“Fuck off” Jim yelled at the room, he looked around searching for the camera in the room and flipped it the middle finger when it he found it. The coffee was ready and he defiantly took it and sat at the table, in the space of a few minutes he’d gone from deflated to angry.
The beeping hadn’t stopped, if anything it had somehow got louder. He sipped at his coffee, he was to annoyed to enjoy it, the beeping from the server room was piercing his head.  It was that is was loud enough to hurt him, it was what is represented; another person who wanted to control him.
He sat at the table eyes closed in a battle of wills to see who would break first, the terminal that was simply running a command to beep, or his fragile state of mind. He lost.
Jim stomped into the server room and sat down at the terminal, nothing had change since he stormed out. The chat was still open exactly where he left it.
[Jim, you don’t think we have surveillance in every room there? I waited for you finish your breakfast before messaging you]
Jim typed [What do you want?]
The answer wasn’t immediate in coming, like the person on the other end had left the room too. Jim waited, soon enough they answered.
[Sorry Jim, I stepped away when I saw you were going to be sitting on the stairs for a bit]
Screw you thought Jim, he typed again [What do you want?]
[I have information you need to know, John is not telling you the truth]
Jim already knew that, he responded [Sure, John’s a liar, tell me something new, better yet let me out of here or just leave me alone]
[Jim, I can’t let you out, I don’t have access for that, I’m risking a lot just by chatting to you]
[Ok, what are you dying to tell me them] asked Jim
[John works for Nova Corp, this is not a Grey safe-house. Nova has these data centres across the city sucking up citizen’s data illegally. Do you really thing the Grey’s have the resource for a bunker like this]
The news that he was trapped here by Nova Corp rocked Jim back in his chair, he sucked in a deep breath. He didn’t have the energy to do much else. Of course it made sense now.
[Nice story, prove it] typed Jim.
A screen shot of an employment file popped into the chat. Jim new what these looked like, it seemed very real. 
There was John, actually his real first name, an employee of Nova Corp for the past five years, a Level 7 executive, he was a Special Projects Manager, whatever that meant. 
Jim read further, John was formerly a CEB intelligence officer, who clearly jumped ship for the private sector, Jim couldn’t blame him for that.
[Why are you showing me this?] asked Jim.
[Because Nova is using you, has been all along, everything that has happened to you is thier doing.]
[What do you mean?]
The answer was a video file.
Jim clicked on it.
His stomach dropped, tt was the interior of the Apex restaurant, just what Jim wanted to see, the moment he’d screwed his whole life up. Except the angle was different from the video that had been released online, this video was clearly from a security camera and showed the the whole booth along with the two each side.
The video showed Rob was already sitting at the booth with the timestamp at 12:01pm, almost thirty minutes before Jim had arrived in the car Rob had booked for him. 
Jim did not like where this was going.
Back in the video, Rob had now taken something out of his jacket, turned around in his seat and started fiddling around at the back of the seat. He got up and sat on the other side of the booth where Jim had sat and pulled out his mobile phone. He was looking at it and then looking across the table. He got up and leaned over to the back of his seat where he looked to be adjusted something, he sat back in Jim’s seat looked at the phone again, nodded and went back to his seat.
It suddenly dawned on Jim what he had just seen; Rob had placed a camera in the seat facing where Jim was going to sit, he even sat in Jim’s seat and adjusted it to make sure the angle was right. 
Fuck me thought Jim.
Jim typed frantically [Was that a camera he hid in the seat to film me, what the hell is going on?]
[Keep watching]
The video started again.
Rob was not talking to the waitress Jim had abused, they were have a good chat by the looks of it, pity there was no sound. Rob took out what looked like Jim’s contract and placed in on the table, just like he had when he offered Jim the job at Nova. Jim could see Rob gesturing with and empty glass to the waitress, he was showing here how he wanted her to spill wine over the contract. They both laughed and Jim saw Rob slipped the waitress his card which she swiped on her personal wallet.
He fucking paid her to spill the wine’ thought Jim. Suddenly he felt light headed and he needed to close his eyes and brace himself against the table to save himself form passing out. He took a few deep breaths and opened his eyes.
He typed the next question in a sort of out of body state. 
[The whole thing was a set-up, was there ever a job, is Rob even real?] 
[To answer your questions, yes the Nova Corp job offer was a set-up from start to finish, there never was a job for you. Rob is real, he works at Nova, he’s actually John’s direct boss]
This was just all too much, it felt like Jim’s whole world had shifted half a foot to the left, he was so displaced. He knew he had one more question to ask, but he didn’t know if he had it in him to even type it.
No, he thought to himself, they will not break him.
He typed, but he knew the answer [Who does Rob answer to?]
The answer seemed like forever coming, but in reality it only took a split second.
[Shane]
#
Shane, who Jim had known for years, the man who’d helped him countless times navigate the politics and intrigue of the corporate ladder and points system. Shane and Helen, Jim and Sarah, a little team who only a few weeks ago had been planning a holiday to Italy. Shane the man who Jim considered, a friend, a mentor, even kind of a big brother that he’d never had. 
That Shane had betrayed him.
Jim was leaning back in the chair silently, the whirring the of the cooling fans the only sound in the room, he could of taste the scent of the warm electronics and plastic in the back of his mouth. His eyes were closed, his hands clasped behind his head, he was unconsciously rocking gently back and forward.
Jim was remembering. 
Snippets of memories with Shane would flash before his eyes, but the rose coloured glasses were off when he was seeing them now. He could see a sly look here and there from Shane when he didn’t think Jim was looking, or a conversation that was that now Jim could see was a subtle manipulation. At the time Jim had shrugged them off as Shane just being Shane, successful, brilliant and eccentric, Jim was just happy to be in his orbit.
It was all falling into place now for Jim, minutes ago his world had shifted a foot to left, but now he was catching up on his new viewpoint and seeing things very differently.
Shane had played him from the day they had met.
Jim remembered that day clearly now, he was a new Level 7 executive, still finding his way in the corporate world and sitting by himself on a lunch break in one of the food courts that seemed to grow on the bottom levels of most major office towers in the city. He remembered he’d splashed on a fancy plate food to celebrate his new status in life, he couldn’t remember what kind of food it was all these years later. What he did remember was that he was sitting minding his own business savouring his food, when a group of people sat a the table next to him. Thier conversation a little to loud for the food court. Jim looked over and saw the expensive tailored suits and the shiny watches, he picked them as Level 8’s immediately; the pinnacle of the corporate world. 
Shane was speaking to his friends “… that was such a blast last night, we’ve all got to do it again soon, Alan, just don’t tell your wife next time.”
The whole table laughed loudly, Alan blushed but played along with the joke.
“Dont worry Shane, next time it will be top secret”
Shane smiled and nodded “So, what’s good here gents? I’ve got a hard out in thirty-five minutes”
He looked over to Jim and without hesitation asked him” Hey, that looks good, where did you get that?” 
The whole table was now looking at Jim, who had just taken a big bite of food could only point at the restaurant he’d bought his dish from. 
Alan seeming eager to please said “Clearly our friend is enjoying it, I’ll grab us a couple of plates.”
One of the other men chimed in “Too big for me, I’ll see what else is here, be back in a few” He and and Alan got up and left the table leaving Shane by himself.
Shane leaned over to Jim and stuck out his hand “I’m Shane”
Jim had swallowed his food, but it felt like it had gone down sideways, he returned the handshake and managed to, almost normally, say “I’m Jim”
“Nice to meet you Jim, you’re new around here, I haven’t seen you before” said Shane.
“Yes, I started a few weeks ago upstairs” answered Jim.
Shane looked at Jim as if appraising him, finally he said “New to Level 7?”
How did he know? thought Jim, he remembered immediately being embarrassed, he was sure he turned bright red.
Before he could answer Shane said “Don’t worry, it’s not that obvious, I’ve just got a eye for detail.”
Jim asked “What gives it away?”
“It’s the off the rack suit, I mean it’s nice fabric and fits quite well, but it’s not tailored, I can tell by the details. Here, take may card and go down to Thorn & Sons, it’s a few blocks down. Show them my card and they’ll do a cracking deal for you, it might stretch you, but take it from me; looking the part is just as important as doing the job”
Shane handed over his card, it was a sleek dark metal card etched with his name a number and nothing else. He waited expectantly looking directly at Jim with his million credit smile, until Jim realised Shane was waiting for Jim’s card in return. 
Jim handed his card over, it was just a standard issue polymer one, in fact he’d only just got them a few days ago, this was the first one he’d handed out.
“Give me a call in a few weeks when you’ve got the suit, I want to test them and see if they helped you out, don’t forget now” Shane said
At the moment Alan and his colleague returned with plates of food and Shane winked at Jim and turned back to his own table, his conversation with Jim was over.
At the time Jim had been so excited to have a new high level contact that he rushed home early from work and told Sarah. Of course he’d gone out and used up three months of savings to buy bespoke tailored suit, he didn’t want to disappoint Shane.
The memory faded, anger creeping up on Jim like he’d never had before. That was it, the day Shane hooked Jim, everything after that was a lie, a manipulation, fake. 
Fuck you Shane, you’re not getting away with this thought Jim.
He opened he eyes, the chat was still open, it was like whoever was on the other end was just wait patiently for Jim to process all this. 
He leaned forward and typed [Fuck Shane and Nova Corp, they’ll pay for this]
There was no answer.
Jim typed again, a flash of revelation had come to him [You don’t work for them do you? You said John and Rob work at Nova, you never said you did]
Again there was no answer.
[You never would have shown me these videos if you did work at Nova, answer me!]
Finally the answer came back [No, I don’t work for Nova Corp]
Jim asked [Why are you showing me this?]
[I want to bring Nova down]
Jim smiled, his anger twisting it into a grimace and typed [Then lets get to work]

























CHAPTER 19

ROB AND JIM (JIM 10)

John was back at the Nova Corp office building, sitting comfortably knowing Dalton was safely locked away out in the Reach. Like all members of Nova Corp’s Special Projects division, he had a small office on the 37th floor befitting his status as a Level 7 executive. The only concession to what he and the division actually did, was that the whole floor was locked down tight. No one came in or out without permission from the very top, and that was never given.
It wasn’t too bad really, certainly it was a big step up after a decade at the Citizenship Enforcement Bureau where he’d ridden a worn down steel desk in a cold and drafty open plan office. He never had the time to really concentrate there, it was a constant distracting hum of voices; agents chasing down leads or citizens pleading for just one more chance and they’d stay on the right side of the law. They never did and he’d stopped giving second chances pretty quickly. 
Here at Nova he had an office to himself, it was small, but not tiny and it was his own. In keeping with Nova’s policy you could decorate it however you wanted and on this floor there was no budget. John had decided to go for an old school lawyers look, dark faux wood and leather, it was a luxury bolt hole when he need to work on projects, or just shut out the noise. He figured he deserved it after years serving the people, he never once regretted jumping ship to the private sector.
Not that he didn’t still like getting out in the field getting his hands dirty for Nova, but some days it was a hard choice, stay put in comfort and send a junior operator, or schlep outside and muscle some unknowing citizen into playing some small part in one of Nova’s grand projects. 
Jim Dalton wasn’t any ordinary dupe though, he’d proven time and again he could get the better of even one of the best resourced departments, he just wouldn’t stay inside the lines they’d very carefully drawn for him. 
Rob stopped by a poked his head into John’s office.
“You’re back, good, what’s Daltons status?” Rob asked.
“He’s locked up in one of our data scoop bunkers out in the Reach, he’s probably worked out by now he can’t get out, but I’ve stocked it for a least a week so we have time to make out next move.”
“Good work, I’ll come back to you once we know what we want to do with him, I’ve got a call with the boss later and we’ll decide if we need to end the project or not” Rob said.
“Yes Sir, I’ll be ready” responded John in his usual matter of fact way.
“Ok, I’m out to lunch, be back later” said Rob as he walked away without bothering to hear if John answered him of not. John didn’t answer just looked at Rob’s back as he left. If Rob had looked back he would have seen a predatory smile on John’s face as he realised that Rob just didn’t know how to deal with the problem and that was leverage John thought he could use.

#
Rob didn’t go out for lunch, for any number of reasons he needed to be close by his office. Shane had made it clear the Dalton project was getting close to its end game and he needed to be on in the office ready when that call came. He also needed to make sure he could drag the project back from the brink, or at least cauterise the wounds he’s suffered on this one, he hadn’t build up a reputation for getting results by letting people like Dalton do what they wanted. 
He’d called John back in from the field once he’d found and secured Dalton. Rob knew John was a seasoned field operative, street smart and not too concerned with ethics, that’s why Rob recruited him from the CEB ins the first place. The lack of loyalty to anything other than himself was always going to be a problem for Rob eventually, he could see it on day one. Recently Rob got the sense that John was looking to move up up from the field and play an altogether different game, a game that Rob was better at. Rob needed a day or so to put a failsafe in place if John was going to make a move on him, but he wasn’t sure he had that much time. He just needed to play out a few scenarios a bit longer before he felt safe.
He’s stopped at the 30th floor executive dining room, it was mostly empty, but that was normal. One of the perks being a Level 9 and a head of the secretive Special Projects division meant he could eat top end restaurant quality meals in the dining room, for free, without any subordinates, or anyone he didn’t want to bothering him.
Rob sat at one of his favourite tables, this one he liked to sit at when he was contemplating and planning. The table was right next to the huge panoramic glass wall overlooking the city, he could see clear out to the hills bordering the eastern edge of the city. The hills where Shane and other CEO’s lived and worked in the Enclave, a secret and secure place where the real power of the city lay, only occasionally did they venture into the city to mix with the citizens. Rob was lucky enough to travel into the Enclave often to meet with Shane at the palatial head quarters of Nova Corp, that also served as Shane primary residence. 
Sitting at the table, the view always reminded Rob of his aim to be out there as a CEO one day, he always felt the view sharpened his thinking and focussed his ambition, he needed that today.
The staff in the dining room knew not to bother him until he signalled, so they kept out of sight. Rob placed his tablet on the table and set it to scan the news feed for the latest ebb and flow of public opinion on the Dalton story. There was a distinct downturn in the news feeds, the story was being pushed off the front page now that Dalton had been missing for days, the news cycle didn’t like when there was no news.
Rob signalled to the staff to bring his lunch, they knew what he wanted already, he sat and waited quietly looking out the window, one ear on the tablet feed, his mind wandering over different scenarios and outcomes.
Within in minutes his food arrived, as always it was an immaculately prepared steak sandwich. The steak was real, no vat soy analogues here, the bread freshly baked, the cheese real dairy and tomatoes and lettuce fresh and crisp. Just what he needed.
Rob was part way through the meal when and alert flashed across his tablet from the New’s scraper he’d been running. 
“NEW ALERT DALTON”
He clicked the alert and he was taken to a dark site that scraped and reviewed any uploaded footage well before it was published, courtesy of Nova’s secret data scooping sites.
He pressed play.
Immediately he could see it was Jim Dalton, he was sitting in the bunker John had left him in, the servers serving as a menacing backdrop.
Fuck! 
Rob took out his earbuds so no one could hear the video, not that anyone would be listening in the dining room, each table was far enough away from each that conversations weren’t easily overheard, the dark wood panelled partitions topped with plants that separated the tables caught any stray words pretty effectively.
Jim was sitting looking directly into the camera and started talking.
“My name is Jim Dalton and I am alive and well, there is not need to concern yourself with my safety. I’m making this video as I am in possession of new information that will exonerate me completely from all the false allegations made against me.”
Rob shut off the video with Dalton mid-sentence, he didn;t have time to watch the rest of item, he had to leave now. His steak sandwich half eaten, he stood up and strode out of the Executive Dining Room without a word, he knew what this meant and he knew the consequences for him and Dalton were dire. 
He had to get to him before anyone else could, already a new scenario was playing out in his mind as he hit the elevator button for the ground floor.

#
John was sitting in his office his feet up on the desk leaning back and snoozing. He didn’t have much to do, the only project on his plate was Dalton and he was tucked away safely for now. The only thing John had to do was wait for Rob to make a decision on what to do next, he could have told him what to do, but John would rather watch Rob screw this up all by himself.
His dozing was interrupted by an alert chime, he cracked his eyes on to see what it was.
“NEW ALERT DALTON”
The data scoops had flagged something new for Dalton, he sat back up and clicked the alert, it linked through Nova dark site that had a video and data on the upload. John read the dat first to get a sense of what is was, often these were just innocuous uploads that he didn’t need to bother with. 
Not this one though, it had been uploaded directly to the Channel 7 tips server, which annoyingly meant he couldn’t pull it back and bury it if he needed to. Worth looking at then, he opened the video.
It was that fucking Jim Dalton, and he was sitting in the server room at the bunker, what the hell was he doing? 
John was fully awake now, shot of adrenalin coursing through his as the gravity of what he was seeing dawned on him, he listened intently.
“My name is Jim Dalton and I am alive and well, there is not need to concern yourself with my safety. I’m making this video as I am in possession of new information that will exonerate me completely from all the false allegations made against me.”
“First I need to tell you that the Greys are not responsible for this, the people who set me up are Nova Corp themselves. That’s right the corporate titans who we all look up are no above ruining a citizen’s life for thier own profit.”
“Don’t take me word for it, here’s the evidence”
The video switched to a view of the Apex Resturant, not the view that Rob and John had released, but a view from a security camera with a full view of the booth Rob and Dalton had lunch of that fateful day’
John watched the video in stunned silence, he saw Rob set up the camera they used to set up Dalton, he saw Rob pay the waitress to spill the wine. How did this video even exist, he was sure he scrubbed the restaurant’s security system throughly.
Dalton was back on screen
“What you just saw was Nova Corp executive Rob Hughes deliberately setting up the restaurant and staff to take me down. Why? I don’t know. Perhaps the people at the top don’t like the idea of someone form the Outer working thier way up to the top, or perhaps they just like playing with people. Either way you should ask Nova Corp CEO Shane Eldridge.”
The video ended.
John sat stock still, this was very bad. Dalton had just out Rob, which he could deal with, be he also outed Shane, which would set off a shit storm of epic proportions.
The desk phone rang, John answered it.
“Did you see that alert?” it was Shane.
“Yes sir, just now” John answered.
“Burn project 29 completely, I want nothing to come back to us, ever. That includes Hughes, understood” Shane said with just a hint of annoyance breaking his usual veneer of cheerfulness.
“Understood sir” 
The line went dead immediately.
#
John sprung into action, his lethargy of the previous few hours had been shattered by Shane’s clear directive; terminate Jim Dalton and Rob Hughes. This was a serious as it could get, but it was the opportunity John was hoping for. Rob had screwed up so badly he was getting burnt along with the subject. He relished the though of it actually, whilst Rob had recruited him into Nova, he’s grown to resent him, Rob treated him like a street enforcer when he had so much more to offer. As for Dalton that guy was just a total pain is the arse and he more than happy to shut him up.
He’d have to move fast, if he could catch Rob in the building, he could take him down to the basement where the Special Projects division had secure, and soundproof rooms.
John burst out of the lift on the 30th floor into the Executive dining room, the Manager tried to stop him, Level 7 did have access to this floor. John shoved him out of way and ran past the private rooms into the large dining room. He scanned the whole room and it was empty, John turned and barked at the waiter who was standing stock still gawking at him “Rob Hughes, where is he?”
The waiter pointed to the windows where a few table were obscured from view by dark wooden partitions topped with plants that spilled over the edges in cascade of lush green. “He’s sitting in the middle table, I just served him lunch, are you supposed to be here?”
John ignored the waiter and bolted over to the window to find and empty seat, but not an empty table. Rob’s half eaten steak sandwich still on the plate, John put the back of his hand onto, it was still warm. Damn Rob must have seen the video too and just bolted. John had to give him grudging credit for understand the situation so quickly.
The manager had caught up with him and started dressing him down.
“Sir you are not authorised to be in the area, this is restricted for Level 9 executives only. Please leave immediately.” John could see two security guards making their way into the dining room.
He didn’t have time for this, Rob could still be in the building. “Here’s my card, write me up later” He pushed past, but the security guards blocked him. John didn’t fancy fighting his was past these two.
He stopped before they could grab him and snatched the card away from the Manager’s hand and passed it over to one of the guards. 
“John Kessler, Senior Agent, Special Projects division, look me up” said John. 
Most of the time he avoided name dropping, but everyone at Nova knew about the Special Projects division, though now one outside the division knew what they did. It was mostly rumours and legends, and its was well known SPD agents had a direct line to Shana.
The guard verified his ID on his tablet and handed the card back.
“Anything we can help you with Mr Kessler?” He asked full of due reverence. 
“Get the fuck out on my way is what you can do” John pushed past them and headed for the lift. 
He pressed the button for the carpark and saw the two guards and the Manager still starting as the doors closed. Fucking idiots though John.
John knew exactly where Rob parked his car, he’d been in his car many times over the years. The car was still sitting quietly in it’s allocated car spot, still plugged in to its charger. He hadn’t left in his car then.
John rummaged around in his jacket and pulled what looked like a small black puck about and inch in diameter and half as thick. He leant over the front of the car and dropped it onto the hood, just above where the console was inside, it stuck like a magnet and beeped once. The device would lock the car down until John released it, Rob was not getting out of here in that.
He was already on the way back up to the 37th floor where the SPD had it’s tactical ops centre that was plugged into all the feeds from the city; he’d find Rob pretty quickly. when his hone rang, it was Shane.
“Update”
“Yes Sir, Hughes must have seen the video at the same time as we did. He’s bolted, left his car behind, left half his lunch behind too.” John couldn’t help to dig at Rob.
“I’m headed to the Ops room to track him down right now”
“No John, Hughes is not first priority, first priority is to stop Dalton from doing anymore damage, then you can join up the search for Hughes. I’ve got other agents on him now.”
“Yes Sir” John answered.
“Oh and John, don’t fuck this up, I’m not in the mood to burn a second staff member today”. The line went dead as usual. 
John had no intention of joining Rob today, but the treat still made him shiver.

#
Rob knew he only had one chance to get out of this alive, and it was a fifty-fifty chance at best. When he bolted from the Nova Corp building, he knew exactly where he needed to go. He calculated he had maybe ten or twenty minutes head start before they realised where he was going. 
Rob knew what happened to his predecessor in the SPD, because he did it. Head of the division wasn’t a position you retired from, eventually you got retired. Knowing this, he’d set up a series of back-ups and contingencies from day one in case he ever had to run, just like we was today.
He’d arrived at the data scoop bunker where Neil was holed up in a public taxi using a fake citizenship profile, that was the easy part, he needed to see if the profile would open the door.
Rob held his breath as he swiped the door, if it didn’t open he knew had to leave the city completely, perhaps never to come back. If it did he’d have to deal with Jim, the man who’s life he ruined.
The door clicked open and Rob pushed it open a silently as possible and snuck down the stairs. he knew the layout of these bunkers, he’d helped design them when he first took over the SPD.
This was it, with a deep breath he open the door into the main room. Jim was standing in the middle of the room holding a large kitchen knife.
“Rob fucking Hughes, the prick who set me up and ruined my life. I never thought I’d get the chance to exact revenge on you, but here you are of all people to turn up in this little Nova Corp jail you’ve got me locked in” Jim said, Rob could tell he was on the verge of losing it completely, the kife was shaking with pent up fury.
Rob put his hand up to stop Jim from coming any close “Wait Jim, you’ve got every right to be angry with me, but that knife isn’t going to help, we’ve got bigger issues right now.”
“We, we don’t have any issue, right now you have the issue, and its convincing me not to stab you right here and now you piece of shit” Jim advanced towards Rob slowly.
Rob backed away and put the small sofa between them.
Still holding his hands up Rob said “Jim, there’ll be time for you to get revenge on me later, right now the full force on Nova corp with be on the way here to kill you and make everything you’ve touched disappear.”
Jim paused “What do you mean aren’t you some Nova Corp bigwig”
“As soon as you dropped that last video, I joined you as a problem to be killed off, we don’t have much time someone will be here any minute know we’ve got to get out.” Shane said. 
Was he getting through to Jim? he didn’t know.
Jim kept the knife point at Rob, but stopped moving forward. “How did I know any of this is true? You, Shane and Nova have been lying to me from the very beginning. Maybe I get out here and leave you to your friends.”
“That won’t help, they won’t stop until they find you. you’ll be on the run forever.” said Rob. 
“I’ve done fine on my own so far” countered Jim.
“From where I’m standing you’re trapped in a Nova Corp bunker with me and any minute now Nova agents will come in that door and kill both of us” Rob said;
He didn’t have time to go around in circles with Jim, he had to make the pitch and it needed to land thought Rob, he continued “More importantly Jim, I’m a Level 9 at Nova, I know exactly what we did and why we we did it. That information is gold for you Jim, you can take it to your friends in the Greys, or keep it to yourself, doesn;t matter to me, but if you let them kill me, you’ll never know why.”
Rob could see that hooked Jim, the knife dropped as he thought about it. 
Now to push the point “Jim, if we get out of here, right now, I’ll tell you whatever you want, but we’ve got to move!” Rob said with a real sense of urgency, he could almost feel Nova breathing down his neck.
Just at that moment they both heard the outer door open.
Rob simply said “It’s them”




















CHAPTER 20

KINETIC (JIM, ROB, JOHN 1)

Jim reacted first, the door to the stair was still open, he called out “John, is that you?”
He gestured frantically for Rob to go into the server room and hide himself. Jim wasn’t sure if Rob was telling the truth or not, but just in case he didn’t want John to see him.
No sooner had Rob ducked in the server room, when John hit the bottom of the stairs. Jim was watching from the middle of the room, the knife hastily tucked into his pants behind his back.
John glanced for a moment at the open door to the main room that was normally shut. “Yes Jim, who else would it be?” he asked lightly.
Jim didn’t answer immediately he subtly shifted across the room as Jonh walked over to the kitchenette, keeping the couch in between them.
“So Jim, want to tell me what the fuck you were doing with that video?” Jim asked, still keeping the light tone, but this time his eye betrayed him, he was anything but friendly. He poured a cup from the coffee pot and sat at the small table.
Jim stayed where he was, he didn’t answer, not because he didn’t want to, but because he didn’t know what to say.
“Not going to answer? Ok then, we’ll do it this way” John pulled out a sleek dark semi- automatic pistol and placed it on the table in front of him the end of the barrel pointing at Jim.
He continued “I’m going to ask some questions, and you are going to answer each one of them without hesitation and you need to be telling me the truth.”
Jim was frozen in his spot, what good would a couch and knife against the pistol,
John took Jim’s silence as confirmation he understood.
“Now I’m a good guy and I genuinely want to help you out, so if everything you say is the truth and helps me out, there’s a chance you could get out of this alive. I’ve got to tell you the guys upstairs don’t care one way or the other.” He sipped at his cup, savouring the flavour.
The mention of John’s bosses brought Jim out of his state “You mean Shane? Fuck Shane, fuck you and Nova” Jim spat out.
John chuckled “We’ll I can’t say I disagree, but seeing as they pay me, quite well actually, I’m going to have to start getting some answers.”
“Firstly, how did you get the security video of Rob at Apex?
Did Jim tell him the truth that an anonymous source contacted him via the terminal in the server room know Rob was hiding in there. That didn’t feel right.
“I’ve had it for days on data stick, the Greys gave it to me when I was with them, I just needed a way to get it out.” said Jim and confidently as he could.
John was quiet for a moment taking it Jim’s word but scrutinising Jim standing there at the same time, he spoke up “Ok Jim, that’s good, see ho easy this can be, if the rest of out chat goes like this I think we can convince Shane to keep you around”
Jim didn’t believe a word John said, he needed to come up with a plan quickly, or he was dead.
John spoke again “Next question Jim, how did you get the video out?”
This was it, the play he needed “I sent it out via the one of the terminals in there” he pointed to the server room”
John frowned “Now Jim, what did I say about lying to me, you’re no hacker and I need to wrap this up, I’ve got other people to see today” he said cryptically.
“No, no, it’s true, the Greys were training me to hack terminals when I was with them, I’m no expert but I can get out a closed system like this… I can show you” He said with just the right amount of desperation in his voice, he hoped.
John nodded, stood up and picked up the pistol, the barrel never straying from pointed directly at Jim “Ok, show me.” he said.

#
Jim walked slowly over to the server room hoping he’d give Rob a few more second to work out what he was going to do, Jim was not making a move under the barrel of John’s pistol, it was on Rob now.
He went inside the room, the door had been left open there hadn’t been enough time to close it before John came into the main rom. That John could see into the room the whole time must have made him confident Jim was alone.
Jim almost froze again when he got inside the room was empty, John who had been three steps behind him, poked the barrel into Jim’s back and gruffly said “Get on with it.”
Jim panicked as he sat down at the first terminal, was Rob even there? Had he imagined the whole thing? He was absolutely sure there was no way out, it took all Jim’s resolve not to look around.
Jim said as nonchalantly as he could muster “Give me a few seconds to get into the system.” 
He started tapping away in what he hoped look like some sort of terminal operating script, John was watching over his shoulder.
Neither of them saw Rob sneak out from behind one of the fridge-sized server racks, his movements disguised by the hum of the cooling fans. He held a side panel off one of the racks in both hands.
He took two step towards John and lifted the panel high and brought it down on the back of John’s head will all the force he could gather. John must have felt the air whoosh against him as the panel dropped down and turned at the last moment. The panel hit a glancing blow on the side of John’s head, it still bent in half and was jolted out of Rob’s grasp, such was the force of the blow.
John staggered to his left further into the room, one hand instinctively going to his head, the other however still held the pistol. He yelled out “What the fuck! who was that?”
Rob stood and watched John stagger around, he’d hadn’t planned for John to still be standing. 
Jim could see Rob wasn’t moving and burst of the chair to tackle John to the floor. He yelled to Rob “Grab the gun!” as he and John fell to the floor.
Rob pounced onto John’s arm that was still holding onto the gun despite having ben hit over the head and tackled to the floor. He grabbed John’s wrist with both hands and slammed John’s hand to the floor, he didn’t let go, but the pistol did. A single shot discharged reverberating loudly in the room, everyone’s ears rang painfully with the sound. The bullet went directly into one of the servers and the metal shards being ejected out the other side into the walls behind.
Jim was wrestling with John hoping to keep him on the floor and subduing him, but he was losing the battle. John was using his free hand to punch Jim in the side repeatedly. He could hit hard, Jim was already winded, he didn’t know how long he could last. 
John was yelling at the both of them as the three of them scrabbled around on the floor “Get off me you arseholes, you’re both dead, you’re not leaving this room.” 
Jim yelled at Rob again “Get that gun” Rob stood up and and stomped on John’s hand, John yelled in pain as Rob broke half the bones in his hand with that one stomp, but it did the trick, he let go of the gun. 
Rob getting up to stomp the hand released the pressure of two men on top of John and Jim lost the battle to keep hold of him. John pushed Jim off and and stood up holding his broken had down to one side. 
He grabbed with his good hand and Rob and brought him in close “I’ve always wanted to do this, you prick” he sneered as be brought his head back and planted a swift headbutt onto the ridge of Rob’s nose, breaking it immediately. Rob staggered backwards, blinded as blood poured out of his nose.
John whipped around to see Jim was now back on his feet and he lunged for him, grabbing for this throat. The forward movement stopped suddenly as John froze, his hand was on Jim’s neck, but it wasn’t squeezing. They both looked down, Jim was holding the handle of the kitchen knife, he had tucked into the back of his pants. The blade was buried deep into John’s abdomen. 
John crumbled to the floor, the fight was over. Jim stood and surveyed the room, all of the furniture had been pushed around as if the room had done a spin cycle, John was barely conscious cradling the knife still stuck in him, Rob was slumped against one of the server racks, his face in his hands, at least the bleeding looked to have stopped. The terminals were bleeping with all sorts of alarms and server rack that got shot was now starting to smoked, the acrid smell of burning circuits was filling the room.
Jim, ignoring John, grabbed to pistol from under one of the upturned chairs and help Rob to his feet. “I think we should get out here” he said his voice shaking as the adrenalin left his system.
Rob only nodded and they walked out of the server together leaning on each for support and leaving John to his fate.
#
Within minutes, Jim and Rob were sitting in the back of an Autocab. Rob had used his burner I.D. to pay for it so they were safe for now. They had time for Rob to splash water on his face and grab a spare shirt from the supplied before the bolted out of the bunker.
According to the console, they had twenty or so minutes to get to thier destination. Jim wasn’t sure where Rob was taking them, right now anywhere away from the bunker was good with him. 
Neither of them had spoken since the fight with John. It was all Jim could think about, the sudden brutal violence and the aftermath. He held his hand so Rob wouldn’t see them shaking. He didn’t want to speak and give voice to what he was thinking; how they left John semi-conscious and bleeding it a room rapidly filling with smoke. Jim might be able to justify what he did to defend himself, but leaving the man for dead was not sitting well with him at all.
Rob was sitting with his head back, a towel from the bunker pressed up against his nose, although it had stopped bleeding. He eyes were closed and he was making no move to tell Jim where they were going. Jim followed suit and closed his eyes, the autocab would get them where they were going without any input from them.
Jim must have dozed and Rob nudged him awake.
“Where here” Rob said.
Jim looked out the window, they were back in city centre. 
Alarmed, he asked Rob “What are we doing back here?”
“Relax, I’ve got a safe site here I set up years ago… just in case, I have days like today. We’ll stop here for the night, get ourselves cleaned up and then get the hell out of the city tomorrow” Rob said, his eyes were starting to swell in the aftermath of John’s headbutt.
Jim moved to open the door, but Rob grabbed him and simply said “Wait.”
Rob reached into his jacket pocket pulling out what looked like the white ping pong ball on the ned of a short stick. He pressed a button on the bottom, a small green indictor light flashed on the side of the stick.
“This will blind all the cameras that can see us, but it only last about 30 seconds before it discharges, so when we get out we need to be through the foyer and into a lift before than happens, otherwise Nova will find us. I’m going to need to hold this at eye level, so you need to swipe this pass to open to doors and call the lift” Rob said as he handed a white access card over to Jim.
“What about inside the building?” Jim asked.
“Taken care of ages ago, Ready?”
Jim nodded and prepared himself for bolt into the building.
Ok, stay close to me, lets go… now!”
Jim slammed the autocab door open, and they both burst out of the door and sprinted across the footpath to the front door, Jim swiped the access card and they were both through the doors before they finished opening. Rob was holding the white ball of the stick right next to thier faces the whole time.
Thankfully, the lift doors opened as soon as they got to them and they scrambled in. The doors closed, the lift has automatically selected thier floor, the 12th. 
Rob let out a big sign of relief as if they were safe now, Jim didn’t, he been promised safety before and so far it have never turned out to be real.
#
The apartment was a simple small one bedroom unit located in a nondescript residential building on the fringes of the city.
